  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖通用视觉嵌入（如 CLIP），难以捕捉艺术图像中由抽象概念与视觉隐喻引发的复杂情感线索，因此亟需一种能模拟人类逐层感知过程的专用框架来提升艺术情感理解能力。

Method: 提出 ProFocus 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 层次化艺术评论家（HAC），利用多模态大语言模型在三个认知层级（氛围风格、叙事主题、具体细节）生成结构化语言先验；(2) 渐进提示融合（PHF）模块，将这些分层提示顺序注入视觉特征，实现类似人类感知的渐进聚焦过程。

Result: 在 ArtEmis v1.0 与 v2.0 数据集上的实验表明，ProFocus 在情感识别和情感解释任务中均显著优于当前最优方法，能够更准确地捕捉细微情感线索并生成更具可解释性的结果。

Conclusion: ProFocus 通过模拟人类对艺术作品的分层认知过程，实现了对艺术图像情感体验的更精准建模，为构建具备情感智能的艺术理解系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Interpreting the emotional responses triggered by images is central to achieving emotional intelligence. Compared with natural images, visual art is intentionally created to elicit emotional responses from its viewers through abstract concepts and visual metaphors, making affective interpretation particularly challenging. However, most existing methods rely on general-purpose visual embeddings (e.g., CLIP), failing to capture the nuanced cues underlying artistic emotion. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{ProFocus}, a novel framework that models affective experience in artistic images via progressive visual focusing. The key idea is to model visual representation learning inspired by a hierarchical cognitive theory of human aesthetic appreciation. Technically, ProFocus contains two core components: a Hierarchical Art Critic (HAC) and a Progressive Hint Fusion (PHF) module. HAC leverages multimodal large language models to generate structured linguistic priors at three cognitive levels--atmospheric style, narrative subjects, and concrete details--thereby translating artistic perception into coherent semantic guidance. Building upon these priors, PHF departs from conventional cross-modal fusion by sequentially injecting the hierarchical hints into visual features, enabling a progressive focusing process that mirrors human perception. This design allows the model to capture subtle affective cues and produce more faithful explanations. Extensive experiments on the ArtEmis v1.0 and v2.0 datasets demonstrate that ProFocus consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both emotion recognition and affective explanation. Project page: https://github.com/Zhang-Zhiyan/ProFocus.

</details>


### [24] [FIRM: Fine-Grained Intra-Token Representation of Masks for Remote Sensing Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13980)
*Weidong Tang,Kaiyu Li,Yikai Wang,Yanan Wu,Haotian Gan,Shihong Wang,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIRM提出细粒度的视觉令牌内掩码表示，通过预测$r\times r$二值子单元模式而非单一前景/背景标签，以保留遥感图像中小目标、细结构和相邻实例的内部空间结构。该方法在一次掩码传递中预测完整网格的掩码代码，并通过固定查找生成离散子单元掩码或通过分布边缘化获得软结构场。此外，引入轻量级连续渲染器利用预合并视觉特征和图像细节进一步细化边界。在五个基准测试中表现领先，显著提升分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在遥感图像中，小目标、细结构和相邻实例可能分布在同一个视觉令牌的不同部分，而传统方法为每个视觉令牌分配单一二值掩码标签，会丢失其内部空间结构，导致目标合并和边界粗糙。因此需要更精细的令牌内表示来捕捉这些复杂的空间关系。

Method: FIRM为每个视觉令牌预测一个掩码代码，表示一个$r\times r$的二值子单元模式，而非单个前景/背景标签。通过一次掩码传递预测整个网格的掩码代码，使用固定查找转换为离散子单元掩码，或通过边缘化代码分布生成软结构场。进一步引入轻量级连续渲染器，结合预合并视觉特征和图像细节，对结构场进行精细化调整，以恢复子单元内的细粒度边界。

Result: 在五个推理与指代分割基准（包括卫星和无人机图像）上，FIRM取得领先性能，如在LaSeRS上达到70.5/80.5的gIoU/cIoU，EarthReason上平均提升3.0点，证明了显式建模令牌内掩码模式对于细粒度多模态大模型分割的重要性。

Conclusion: FIRM通过显式建模视觉令牌内部的细粒度掩码结构，有效解决了遥感图像中因令牌聚合导致的目标融合与边界模糊问题，在多个基准上实现了显著性能提升，展示了细粒度内令牌表示在多模态大模型分割中的关键价值。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to translate implicit instructions into precise pixel-level masks. MLLMs encode an image as visual tokens, each of which merges a group of image patches. In remote sensing images, small targets, thin structures, and adjacent instances can occupy different parts of the same visual token. Assigning a single binary mask label to such a token loses its internal spatial structure, causing nearby targets to merge and object boundaries to become coarse. To bridge this representational gap, we introduce FIRM, a Fine-grained Intra-token Representation of Masks. For each visual token, FIRM predicts a mask code that specifies an $r\times r$ binary sub-cell pattern rather than a single foreground/background label. Given a target identified by the MLLM, the complete grid of mask codes is predicted in one mask pass. Fixed lookup converts the predicted codes into a discrete sub-cell mask, while marginalizing the code distribution yields a soft structural field. To further recover fine-grained boundaries within each sub-cell, we introduce a lightweight continuous renderer that refines this field using pre-merge visual features and image details. Across five reasoning and referring segmentation benchmarks on satellite and UAV images, FIRM achieves leading results, including $70.5/80.5$ gIoU/cIoU on LaSeRS and a $3.0$-point average gain on EarthReason. These results demonstrate the value of explicitly representing intra-token mask patterns for fine-grained MLLM segmentation.

</details>


### [25] [MedClaw: Heuristic Agent Harness for Long-Horizon Surgical Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14015)
*Yingying Fan,Penghui Du,Leyan Zhu,Runze He,Zimeng Wu,Yuxuan Zhang,Liang Chen,Jiahao Xie,Jiangtang Wang,Shuai Shao,Anchao Yang,Yutong Bai,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种新型手术视频理解框架，通过分离推理与感知，利用文本驱动的调度器规划证据收集，并调用冻结的视觉语言子代理执行具体操作（如视图、裁剪、帧检查和外部知识检索）。采用无梯度、基于奖励的启发式技能蒸馏循环，从低分轨迹中挖掘可复用的检索技能（如定向重看），仅需约100个标注样本即可适应新任务，显著减少数据需求。为评估该方法，构建了MedClawBench基准，包含1,123个医生标注的问题，覆盖自建神经外科长视频和公开讲座视频测试集。在所有评估维度上，该代理均优于单次输入的视觉语言模型和通用视频代理框架，尤其在长时序、跨领域神经外科视频上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理数十分钟级手术视频时存在局限：单次输入的视觉语言模型因上下文窗口限制压缩整个手术过程，丢失前后关系细节；而依赖训练注意力机制的视频代理数据需求大且泛化能力差。因此需要一种能有效进行长时程时间推理、兼具高效性与可迁移性的新方法。

Method: 构建一个由文本调度器与冻结视觉语言子代理组成的代理系统。调度器生成可审计的工具调用序列，子代理在像素层面执行具体操作（如查看、裁剪、检测、检索）。引入梯度无关的奖励门控启发式技能蒸馏循环，自动筛选并保留提升验证奖励的候选技能，形成可复用的外部技能库，避免参数微调。

Result: 在自建神经外科视频和公开讲座视频上的多维度评估中，该代理全面超越单次输入的视觉语言模型和通用视频代理框架，尤其在长时序、跨域场景下优势显著。仅需约100个标注样本即可完成技能学习，远低于传统监督或强化学习方法所需数据量。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种高效、可迁移、可解释的手术视频理解代理系统，通过分离推理与感知、构建可复用技能库的方式，实现了对长时间手术视频的精准时空推理，为医疗视觉理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Understanding tens-of-minutes surgical videos requires long-horizon temporal reasoning, answering what happens before, after, or across stages of a procedure by grounding the question in visual evidence spread across time. Existing approaches handle this poorly: a one-shot vision-language model (VLM) compresses the whole procedure to fit its context window and loses the detail a "before" or "after" question depends on, while video agents that train the model where to look are data-hungry and transfer poorly to out-of-domain surgery. We build an agent harness that separates reasoning from perception and improves by evolving context rather than optimizing weights. A text-only orchestrator plans which evidence to gather and issues an auditable sequence of tool calls, while frozen vision-language sub-agents execute each call over the pixels, viewing, cropping, inspecting frames, and retrieving external knowledge. We further propose a gradient-free, reward-gated Heuristic Skill Distillation loop that mines the agent's own low-scoring traces and keeps a candidate skill only when it raises a validation reward, yielding reusable retrieval skills, notably directed re-look. Growing an external skill library rather than tuning weights, the loop adapts from only about 100 labeled examples, far fewer than supervised or reinforcement fine-tuning requires. To evaluate this agent, we introduce MedClawBench, a de-leaked, doctor-grounded benchmark of 1,123 questions over self-built long neurosurgery recordings and a held-out public lecture-video test split. Across both datasets and all four evaluation dimensions, our agent consistently outperforms one-shot VLMs and general video-agent frameworks, with the largest gains on the long, out-of-domain neurosurgery videos. Project page: https://fyycs.github.io/medclaw/.

</details>


### [26] [Content Based Video Narration of Gameplay with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14016)
*Mathew Varghese*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内容的视频叙事系统，利用通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）和文本转语音（TTS）后端，为任意游戏录像生成类电子竞技风格的实时解说，无需游戏特定工具、引擎遥测或任务专用训练。系统采用三种机制：时间马赛克打包将九帧均匀采样合并为3x3图像，使图像原生VLM能以单个图像负载推理运动；上下文条件提示通过回放最近K条解说作为历史记录，减少静态场景重复描述；时长条件生成与弹性对齐在提示中控制叙述长度，并通过时间缩放或对称填充音频，确保每段解说精确匹配片段时长，实现帧级精确混音。系统支持云端或本地6位量化40亿参数的Apple Silicon设备TTS模型，实现完全本地化语音合成。实验包括实时战略游戏案例研究，显示马赛克技术使每分钟图像负载降低9倍，同时报告了观察到的失败模式：虚构游戏状态、马赛克导致的分辨率损失以及时间缩放引发的语调失真。系统已开源，提供可复现基线及未来定量评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实时游戏解说资源稀缺，主要局限于专业电竞赛事直播，而大多数游戏录像缺乏解说。现有方法通常依赖游戏特定数据、引擎遥测或专门训练，难以泛化。本文旨在构建一个无需特定游戏配置、不依赖外部信号的通用自动解说系统，提升非专业游戏视频的观赏性与信息传达效率。

Method: 系统采用三重机制：1）时间马赛克打包，将九帧均匀采样合并为3x3图像，使图像原生视觉-语言模型能够以单图像输入感知运动信息；2）上下文条件提示，将最近K条解说作为助理角色历史注入提示，抑制静态画面重复描述；3）时长条件生成与弹性对齐，通过提示控制生成长度，并结合时间缩放或对称填充音频，实现帧级精确对齐，无需强制对齐器。语音阶段支持云端或本地6位量化40亿参数TTS模型，可在Apple Silicon设备上运行。

Result: 系统成功实现了对任意游戏录像的自动生成解说，案例研究表明其在实时战略游戏中的有效性；马赛克技术使每分钟图像处理负载降低9倍；观察到的主要问题包括：虚构游戏状态、因马赛克造成的分辨率下降、以及时间缩放引起的语调异常；系统已开源，提供可复现基线和未来定量评估框架。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一种无需游戏特定训练或外部数据的通用视频解说系统，通过创新的视觉编码与生成策略，在保持帧级精度的同时实现高质量、低延迟的自动解说。尽管存在一些局限性如幻觉和语调失真，但其架构具备良好的扩展性与实用性，为非专业游戏内容的自动化解说提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Live game commentary is scarce: it exists for professional esports broadcasts and almost nowhere else. We present a content-based video narration system that produces spoken, esports-style commentary for arbitrary gameplay recordings using a general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) and a text-to-speech back end, with no game-specific instrumentation, no engine telemetry, and no task-specific training. Three mechanisms carry the system. Temporal mosaic packing arranges nine uniformly sampled frames into a single 3x3 image, letting an image-native VLM reason about motion while consuming one image payload per segment instead of nine. Context-conditioned prompting replays the K most recent narrations as assistant-role history, suppressing the repetition that dominates per-segment captioning of static scenes. Duration-conditioned generation and elastic alignment constrain narration length in the prompt, then time-scale or symmetrically pad the synthesized audio so each utterance fills its segment slot exactly, giving frame-accurate muxing without a forced aligner. The implementation supports either cloud TTS or a 6-bit quantized 4B-parameter on-device TTS model on Apple silicon, making the speech stage fully local. We report a qualitative case study on real-time strategy footage, a cost model showing the mosaic reduces per-minute image payloads by 9x, and a candid account of observed failure modes - hallucinated game state, resolution loss from mosaicking, and prosody artifacts from time-scaling. We release the system as a reproducible baseline, with an evaluation protocol for the quantitative study a full version will report.

</details>


### [27] [ForgeWM: Progressive Causal Training for Few-Step Action-Conditioned Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14022)
*Xinye Li,Lingshuai Lin,Lei Wang,Liuzhou Zhang,Jialin Cui,Qingshan Li,Guanchu Wang,Qingbin Liu,Xi Chen,Jiang Bian,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForgeWM 是一种渐进式框架，通过领域适应、教师强制因果训练、因果一致性蒸馏和基于双向教师的在线策略分布匹配，将双向动作条件视频生成器转化为高效的少步世界模型。该框架支持低延迟交互与可选的回放时间优化，能够在保持高精度动作控制的同时实现高质量视频生成。在 Minecraft 和 FPS 游戏中均表现出色，显著提升图像质量、运动轨迹一致性、动作符号准确性和鼠标控制精度，同时降低参考 LPIPS 值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果蒸馏方法难以扩展到交互式世界模型，因离散键盘状态与连续鼠标运动需与压缩的潜在块在时序上对齐，导致训练与推理复杂性高。需要高效、低延迟且可靠的因果视频生成机制以支持游戏内实时交互。

Method: 采用四阶段方法：领域适应、教师强制因果训练、因果一致性蒸馏、以及基于双向教师的在线策略分布匹配；结合双路径部署策略，实现低延迟交互与回放时的重噪声优化。

Result: 在配对的 Minecraft 轨迹上，ForgeWM 在图像质量、运动轨迹一致性、动作符号准确性和鼠标控制精度方面优于现有系统，参考 LPIPS 最低；回放优化达到四步参考质量，且比从噪声再生更接近真实轨迹约三倍。该方法可迁移至游戏手柄控制的 FPS 游戏。

Conclusion: ForgeWM 有效实现了可控的少步视频生成，在保证低延迟与高保真度的同时，支持精确的动作响应与高质量的视觉输出，适用于多种交互式游戏环境。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models require low-latency causal generation and reliable responses to game-native controls. Although causal distillation enables one- or few-step video synthesis, extending it to interactive world models remains challenging, as discrete keyboard states and continuous mouse motion must remain aligned with temporally compressed latent chunks during causal training and autoregressive rollout. We introduce ForgeWM, a progressive framework that transforms a bidirectional action-conditioned video generator into efficient few-step world models through domain adaptation, teacher-forced causal training, causal consistency distillation, and on-policy distribution matching with a bidirectional teacher. The resulting budget-specialized students operate at steady-state denoising budgets of 1, 2, and 4 steps. ForgeWM further supports a dual-path deployment protocol combining latency-critical interaction with optional replay-time refinement, where the one-step student re-noises and refines its saved draft. On paired Minecraft trajectories, ForgeWM leads the evaluated systems in Imaging Quality, reference-aligned motion-profile agreement, action-sign accuracy, and mouse-control accuracy, while achieving the lowest reference LPIPS; the same four-stage recipe transfers to gamepad-controlled FPS gameplay. Replay-time refinement matches four-step reference quality while remaining roughly three times closer to the experienced trajectory than regeneration from noise. These results demonstrate ForgeWM's effectiveness for controllable few-step video generation.

</details>


### [28] [SSP: An Event-Matched Syn2Sim2Phy Cross-Domain Evaluation Framework for Autonomous Driving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14024)
*Haojie Feng,Peizhi Zhang,Xinrui Zhang,Zhuoren Li,Junpeng Huang,Xiurong Wang,Dongxiao Yin,Yuxiang Zhang,Junfan Zhu,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SSP框架，通过事件匹配的合成-仿真-物理跨域评估，确保安全关键交互的一致性，实现对视觉-语言-动作模型在真实场景中行为的可复现、证据支持的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法使用独立选择的合成、仿真和物理数据，导致性能差距可能由场景内容差异而非真正的领域敏感性引起，缺乏统一基准。

Method: 构建事件匹配的合成-仿真-物理评估框架（SSP），基于长尾合成视频生成验证后的事件规范，并在CARLA和实测场地中重建平台特定实现，通过转移审计确保关键属性保持一致；将多模型输出映射到统一语义槽与1秒轨迹窗口，评估输出有效性、语义准确性、关键交互识别、轨迹质量和风险响应能力。

Result: 在变道和行人穿越等场景下，合成、仿真、物理域的宏观平均整合VLA能力得分分别为0.259、0.291、0.325，最佳域因场景而异；Alpamayo-R1、OpenEMMA、LLaViDA得分分别为0.405、0.338、0.131。

Conclusion: SSP提供可复现的场景迁移链和无假设物理优越性的证据化评估，有效提升跨域评估的可信度与公平性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving jointly produce scene interpretation, language-based reasoning, and driving trajectories. Existing evaluations often use independently selected synthetic, simulated, and physical data, so measured performance gaps can be confounded by changes in scenario content rather than genuine domain sensitivity. We propose SSP (Synthetic-Simulation-Physical), an event-matched Syn2Sim2Phy evaluation framework that anchors cross-domain comparison to the same safety-critical interaction. Starting from a synthetic long-tail video, SSP builds a validated event specification that preserves road topology, participant roles, relative motion, conflict evolution, passing order, response constraints, and event phases. Platform-specific realizations are then constructed in CARLA and on a closed proving ground and are evaluated only after transfer audits confirm preservation of mandatory event properties. SSP maps heterogeneous outputs from OpenEMMA, LLaViDA, and Alpamayo-R1 into common semantic slots and a 1 s trajectory window to assess output validity, semantic accuracy, critical-interaction recognition, trajectory quality, and risk response. Across Cut-in and vulnerable-road-user crossing cases, the macro-averaged Integrated VLA Capability Scores are 0.259, 0.291, and 0.325 in the Synthetic, Simulation, and Physical domains, respectively, while the best domain varies by scenario. Alpamayo-R1, OpenEMMA, and LLaViDA obtain scores of 0.405, 0.338, and 0.131. SSP provides a reproducible scene-transfer chain and an evidence-qualified evaluation of VLA behavior without assuming that the Physical domain is universally superior.

</details>


### [29] [E-S2Feat:Semantic-Guided Spiking Local Feature Detection and Description for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14027)
*Yang Yi,Juntao Hua,Jinpu Zhang,Liangwei Fan,Hui Shen,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出E-S2Feat框架，通过模块化脉冲激活机制和语义引导特征调制机制，提升事件相机下局部特征的表示能力与判别性，在保持高精度的同时实现约4.8倍的计算能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决事件相机中事件稀疏性、噪声和纹理有限导致的特征学习鲁棒性差问题，同时满足资源受限平台（如无人机）对精度与能效的平衡需求。

Method: 设计一种脉冲神经网络框架E-S2Feat，包含模块特定的脉冲激活机制以保留细粒度结构信息，并引入语义引导的特征调制机制，优化关键点响应分布与描述子判别力。

Result: 在ECD和EDS数据集上显著优于SuperEvent等基线方法，在姿态估计精度上表现更优；性能接近传统ANN模型，但理论计算能效提升约4.8倍；在TUM-VIE数据集上的视觉惯性里程计实验验证了其在完整SLAM系统中的有效性与应用潜力。

Conclusion: E-S2Feat有效提升了事件相机下局部特征学习的鲁棒性与效率，为资源受限平台上的高效视觉系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Benefiting from high temporal resolution and dynamic range, event-based local feature methods have attracted increasing attention. However, event sparsity, noise, and limited texture still hinder robust local feature learning. Deploying such methods on resource-constrained platforms such as unmanned aerial vehicles also requires balancing accuracy and energy efficiency. To address these challenges, this paper proposes \textbf{E-S2Feat}, a spiking neural network framework for event-based local feature detection and description. The framework jointly optimizes local feature learning from the perspectives of feature representation and selection. First, a module-specific spiking activation mechanism preserves fine-grained structural cues and discriminative information under low-bit, energy-efficient inference, thereby improving overall representation fidelity. Furthermore, a semantic-guided feature modulation mechanism leverages semantic priors to refine keypoint response distributions and enhance local descriptor discriminability, thereby guiding the model to extract local features with greater geometric stability and stronger discriminative capability. Experiments on the ECD and EDS datasets show that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline methods such as SuperEvent in pose estimation accuracy. It also achieves accuracy comparable to its artificial neural network counterpart while delivering an approximately 4.8-fold improvement in theoretical computational energy efficiency. Visual-inertial odometry experiments on the TUM-VIE dataset further verify the effectiveness and practical application potential of the proposed method in complete SLAM systems.

</details>


### [30] [Beyond Text Conditioning: A Systematic Study of MLLM-DiT Fusion for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14043)
*Yanbo Ding,Yijia Fan,Caihua Shan,Yifan Yang,Yifei Shen,Weijie Wang,Xirui Hu,Dongsheng Li,Lili Qiu,Yuqing Yang,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BiVidGen，一种结合多模态大模型（MLLM）与扩散变压器（DiT）的视频生成框架，通过生成离散语义视觉标记作为中间表示，显著提升文本到视频生成中的语义对齐与时间连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散变压器在视频生成中缺乏高层语义规划能力，虽多模态大模型在图像生成中表现优异，但在视频生成中仍被用作冻结特征编码器，未充分发挥其语义生成潜力。

Method: 系统研究MLLM与DiT的集成方式，探索中间表征选择、MLLM生成策略及DiT条件机制；提出基于EMA-Tokenizer的离散语义视觉标记，采用自回归因果建模生成，并通过多层交叉注意力实现显式视觉标记条件控制。

Result: 实验表明，BiVidGen在语义对齐和时间连贯性上优于微调的DiT基线，在VBench-Long上表现更优，证明显式语义规划是有效且超越纯文本条件的中间接口。

Conclusion: 显式利用多模态大模型进行视觉语义规划，通过离散视觉标记作为中间表示，能够有效提升文本到视频生成的质量，为未来视频生成提供新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant paradigm for high-fidelity video generation, yet their ability to perform high-level semantic planning remains limited. While hybrid architectures integrating MLLMs with diffusion backbones have shown strong advantages in image synthesis, such designs remain underexplored in video generation, where existing approaches often treat MLLMs primarily as frozen feature encoders rather than semantic generators. To fill this gap, we systematically study how an MLLM should be integrated with a DiT for video generation by answering three questions: what intermediate representation should bridge the MLLM and DiT, how the MLLM should generate it, and how the DiT should incorporate it during diffusion rendering. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (1) discrete semantic visual tokens produced by an EMA-based tokenizer provide a stable and expressive interface, (2) autoregressive causal modeling is effective for generating these tokens, and (3) explicit visual-token conditioning is more effective than prompt refinement or latent bridging. Based on these findings, we propose BiVidGen, a hybrid framework where an MLLM first generates semantic visual tokens and a DiT renders videos conditioned on both text and these tokens via multi-layer cross-attention. Extensive experiments show that BiVidGen improves semantic alignment and temporal coherence over a fine-tuned DiT baseline, achieving stronger performance on VBench-Long. These results demonstrate that explicit MLLM-based visual planning provides an effective intermediate interface for text-to-video generation beyond text-only conditioning.

</details>


### [31] [Source-Agnostic Image Translation Based on Latent Aware Adaptive Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14046)
*Tomislav Dobrički,Byung-Woo Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需源图像的动态掩码修正框架，通过分析预训练扩散模型在不同时间步的预测差异，自适应地调整二值掩码。该方法采用随时间变化的统计阈值，基于目标分布中噪声图像的预测差异均值与标准差，使掩码能根据模型在不同噪声水平下的置信度进行调整，从而精确分离领域特定区域并保持全局结构一致性。实验表明，在AFHQ和Celeba-HQ数据集上，该方法在真实性和保真度方面均优于现有无监督图像到图像转换方法。仅需目标域的预训练模型，即可实现对多种源分布的精准、自动化定位与无缝转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督图像到图像转换方法依赖固定阈值或额外训练，难以适应不同噪声水平下的预测不确定性，导致局部细节失真或结构破坏。因此需要一种无需源图像、可自适应调整掩码的动态方法，以提升生成质量与领域特异性控制能力。

Method: 提出一种基于预测差异的时变统计阈值机制，利用预训练扩散模型在反向扩散过程中各潜空间时间步的预测偏差，计算其经验均值与标准差，动态生成时间相关掩码；通过迭代优化掩码，实现对目标领域特征的精准捕捉与保留。

Result: 在AFHQ和Celeba-HQ数据集上，本方法在FID、KID（真实性）以及SSIM、LPIPS（保真度）指标上均优于当前最先进的无监督图像到图像转换方法，且仅需目标域预训练模型，无需针对特定源分布进行微调。

Conclusion: 所提出的源无关动态掩码框架能够有效适应不同噪声水平下的模型预测置信度变化，实现高保真、高真实感的跨域图像编辑，具备良好的泛化性与自动化能力，为无监督图像转换提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a source-agnostic framework that dynamically refines a binary mask throughout the reverse diffusion process by computing the discrepancies of a pretrained diffusion model's prediction for each latent time step. Rather than relying on a fixed threshold, our method introduces a time-dependent statistical thresholding scheme derived from the empirical mean and standard deviation of prediction discrepancies across the latent noisy images from the target distribution. This allows the mask to adapt to the model's varying predictive confidence at different noise levels, effectively isolating domain-specific regions while preserving global structural coherence. Experimental results on the AFHQ and Celeba-HQ datasets demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art unsupervised Image-to-Image methods in both realism (FID, KID) and faithfulness (SSIM, LPIPS). By requiring only a pretrained model of the target domain, our approach enables precise, automated localization and seamless translation across diverse source distributions without any specialized training. The project source code is available at: https://github.com/dtoma95/PM-Edit

</details>


### [32] [Discovery and Spatial Characterisation of Multiple Shortcut Groups for Auditing Vision Model Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14051)
*Akshit Achara,Vishnunarayan Manickam,Thomas Day,Esther Puyol Anton,Alexander Hammers,Andrew P. King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，通过聚类图像级的快捷特征与任务相关贡献图，识别出跨数据集和模型（如ResNet、ViT）中重复出现的空间模式。该方法结合K-means与非负矩阵分解，克服了现有方法在全局聚合时掩盖局部快捷模式的问题。在CelebA、CheXpert、Waterbirds、Camelyon17和ISIC2019等数据集上验证，发现不同子组存在差异化的空间快捷模式与错误率，支持对高错误率图像子集进行靶向分析。进一步通过遮挡和测试时干预证明，抑制任务相关区域会显著降低性能，并提出联合抑制快捷特征、放大任务特征的干预策略，有效缓解性能偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖全局聚合的归因图来识别快捷特征，难以揭示仅在特定图像子集中出现的空间快捷模式，导致对模型偏差机制的理解不充分。需要更精细的局部化分析手段以定位真实快捷关联区域。

Method: 使用条件对齐生成每张图像的快捷对齐与任务对齐贡献图；利用K-means与非负矩阵分解对这些图进行聚类，提取重复出现的空间模式；通过代表性样本与贡献图可视化结果；结合输入遮挡与测试时干预评估模型行为。

Result: 所发现的快捷模式在不同数据集和模型间具有共享与独特性，且与子组构成及错误率密切相关。通过联合干预策略可有效降低性能差异，提升公平性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效识别并可视化深层学习模型中的空间快捷模式，为理解模型偏差来源提供新视角，并支持针对性干预以减少分布外性能差距。

Abstract: Deep learning models trained on datasets with spurious correlations can achieve high average accuracy whilst relying on shortcut features that do not generalise out of distribution. Whilst out-of-distribution testing highlights subgroup performance disparities arising from shortcut learning, it does not localise the regions within images that are associated with it. Existing research mostly uses attribution maps from interpretability methods to understand the spatial nature of spurious correlations. For example, conditional alignment methods separate task-relevant evidence from evidence tied to spurious correlations by comparing attribution maps from a task model, a sensitive attribute model, and a bias-reduced reference model. This yields shortcut-aligned and task-aligned contribution maps for each image. However, existing methods aggregate these maps across the dataset, potentially masking recurring spatial shortcut patterns that occur only in subsets of images. We address this limitation by grouping per-image shortcut and task contribution maps into recurring spatial patterns using K-means and non-negative matrix factorisation, and visualising the resulting shortcut groups through contribution maps and representative examples. Across CelebA, CheXpert, Waterbirds, Camelyon17, and ISIC2019, and across ResNet and ViT models, the discovered shortcut groups reveal both shared and distinct spatial patterns of shortcut and task contribution, with varying subgroup composition and error rates, enabling targeted inspection of image subsets with higher error rates. We perform input occlusion and internal test-time interventions to show that masking or suppressing task contribution regions substantially degrades the model classification performance and propose a combined shortcut suppression and task amplification feature intervention approach which generally reduces performance disparities.

</details>


### [33] [Voxel-based 3D Facies Segmentation from Seismic Data: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14058)
*Duc-Thanh Pham,Minh-Tan Pham,Anh Nguyen,Van Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于体素的3D地震相位分割的可比较且可复现的基准，使用公开的荷兰F3和Parihaka数据集，通过评估三种现代3D分割架构，建立了强基线结果，揭示了该领域潜在机会与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在2D地震相位分割上表现良好，但将3D地震数据预处理为2D切片导致切片间不连续，无法保持3D数据的空间和结构连续性，限制了模型学习连贯地质模式的能力。

Method: 构建基于公开地震数据集（荷兰F3和Parihaka）的体素级3D地震相位分割基准，采用标准化数据划分和评估指标，评估三种代表性3D分割架构。

Result: 建立了强基线性能，验证了3D方法在保留地质结构连续性方面的优势，同时指出了当前技术在复杂地质场景下的局限性。

Conclusion: 3D体素分割方法在地震相位识别中具有显著潜力，但仍需进一步研究以克服现有挑战，如标注数据稀缺和复杂地质结构建模。

Abstract: Seismic facies segmentation has emerged as a significant challenge in geophysics, requiring robust methods and systems to effectively identify geologically analogous facies with limited labeled data. Although existing studies have shown promising results in 2D facies segmentation, they often preprocess the original 3D seismic volumes into sets of 2D slices, typically the inline and crossline directions, and treat this problem as a purely 2D segmentation task. This simplification introduces discontinuities across slices and fails to preserve the spatial and structural continuity in 3D seismic data, thus limiting the model's ability to learn coherent geological patterns. In this work, we present a comparative and reproducible benchmark for voxel-based 3D seismic facies segmentation, built upon publicly available seismic volumes including the Netherlands F3 and the Parihaka datasets, with standardized data splits and evaluation metrics. By evaluating the three representative families of modern 3D segmentation architectures, we establish strong baseline results that highlight the potential and remaining challenges for future research in this domain.

</details>


### [34] [InstructVVT: Instruction-Driven Video Virtual Try-On without Auxiliary Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14070)
*Dingbao Shao,Song Wu,Xinyu Chen,Qian Wang,Jiahang Li,Kuai Jiang,Jiang Lin,Yuhang Liu,Ziyu Chen,Duo Li,Jiaxin Hu,Shengrong Gu,Ziheng Tang,Rongrong Liu,Yanlun Peng,Liang Li,Junlan Feng,Lujia Jin,Ting Zhang,Jian Yang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstructVVT 是一种无需推理时空间先验的指令驱动、参考引导的视频虚拟试穿框架，基于扩散Transformer（DiT），通过双层参考条件机制从输入三元组（源视频、参考服装、指令）中直接恢复细粒度控制，利用多模态大模型（MLLM）生成语义编辑令牌以实现目标消歧和结构保持，并通过轻量级条件路径注入精细服装视觉细节。此外，设计了针对虚拟试穿的人类偏好奖励函数，并采用DiffusionNFT算法对齐人类偏好。在ViViD-S和TripVVT-Bench上的实验表明，InstructVVT在服装保真度、结构保持和时序一致性方面优于现有开源方法，且所需推理控制更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法严重依赖手工设计的空间先验（如掩码、姿态），这些先验在真实世界无约束视频中容易失效，且将丰富的视觉上下文压缩为不完整的结构信号；同时，标准重建目标无法充分捕捉虚拟试穿特有的人类偏好。因此需要一种无需外部先验、能从输入中直接提取细粒度控制的新方法。

Method: 提出InstructVVT框架，基于扩散Transformer（DiT），采用双层参考条件机制：1）使用多模态大模型（MLLM）从输入指令和参考服装中推断语义编辑令牌，用于目标消歧与结构保持；2）设计轻量级条件路径，显式注入服装的细粒度视觉细节。结合专为虚拟试穿设计的奖励函数与DiffusionNFT算法，使模型输出更符合人类偏好。

Result: 在ViViD-S和TripVVT-Bench数据集上，InstructVVT在服装保真度、结构保留和时序一致性方面均优于当前最先进的开源方法，且在推理阶段仅需极少控制输入，显著降低使用门槛。

Conclusion: InstructVVT通过指令和参考引导的方式实现了无需空间先验的高精度视频虚拟试穿，能够有效恢复细粒度控制并匹配人类偏好，在性能和实用性上均有显著提升。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on is a highly constrained editing task requiring the precise replacement of a target person's clothing while strictly preserving the original video's spatial structure and temporal dynamics. Existing methods heavily rely on auxiliary handcrafted spatial priors (e.g., masks, poses) for editing control. However, these priors are prone to failure in unconstrained real-world videos and often compress rich visual context into incomplete structural signals. Furthermore, standard reconstruction objectives fail to fully capture try-on-specific human preferences. To address these challenges, we propose InstructVVT, an instruction-driven and reference-guided video virtual try-on framework based on a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates without inference-time spatial priors. Our core insight is to recover fine-grained control directly from the input triplet (source video, reference garment, and instruction) via a dual-level reference conditioning scheme. Specifically, an MLLM infers semantic edit tokens for target disambiguation and structural preservation, while a lightweight conditioning pathway explicitly injects fine-grained visual garment details. Finally, we design a try-on-specific reward and utilize the DiffusionNFT algorithm to align the model with human preferences. Extensive experiments on ViViD-S and TripVVT-Bench demonstrate that InstructVVT outperforms state-of-the-art open-source methods in garment fidelity, structural preservation, and temporal consistency, despite requiring fewer inference-time controls.

</details>


### [35] [Owner3D: Ownership-Guided Style Writing for Training-Free Localized 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14078)
*Suchang Tao,Kaifeng Shi,Zhiyan Liu,Zhuoyuan Jiang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Owner3D 是一种无需训练的局部 3D 风格化框架，通过引入基于归属的风格写入和边界双槽机制，在大型重建模型中实现精准的局部风格控制，有效避免风格泄漏和边界模糊问题。其表面优先的纹理读取策略进一步提升了不完整可见性下的外观恢复能力。在 Google Scanned Objects 和 PartNet 构建的基准上，显著优于现有方法，风格保真度更高，非目标区域外观保持更佳，风格泄漏分别降低 86.4% 和 89.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型重建模型中，风格注入发生在渲染前的中间外观表示阶段，而紧凑的三平面特征在目标与非目标表面间共享，导致风格泄漏和边界模糊，难以实现精确的局部 3D 风格化。

Method: 提出 Owner3D 框架，通过所有权引导的风格写入限制参考风格仅注入目标区域；引入边界双槽机制，为目标与非目标区域维护独立的局部特征源；采用表面优先的纹理读取策略，分层融合表面、3D 和三平面的归属证据以增强外观恢复鲁棒性。

Result: 在 Google Scanned Objects 与 PartNet 基准上，Owner3D 在目标区域风格保真度和非目标区域外观保留方面均优于现有方法，相比 StyleSplat 和 LAENeRF，风格泄漏分别减少 86.4% 和 89.9%。

Conclusion: Owner3D 实现了无需训练的高效局部 3D 风格化，有效解决了风格泄漏与边界模糊问题，为大型重建模型中的精细外观控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Localized 3D stylization aims to modify the appearance of a specified object part while preserving the remaining surfaces. In large reconstruction models (LRMs), this task is challenging because style is injected into intermediate appearance representations before rendering, while compact triplane features are shared across target and non-target surfaces, causing style leakage and boundary ambiguity. We propose Owner3D, a training-free framework for localized 3D stylization that integrates localized appearance control directly into the LRM reconstruction process. Specifically, Owner3D introduces ownership-guided style writing to restrict reference-style injection to target regions, producing a single localized stylized triplane without additional training while avoiding separate global style and appearance representations. To resolve appearance ambiguity near semantic boundaries, we further introduce boundary dual slots that maintain separate local feature sources for target and non-target regions. Finally, a surface-first texture readout hierarchically combines surface, 3D, and triplane ownership evidence to robustly recover appearance under incomplete visibility. Experiments on a benchmark constructed from Google Scanned Objects and PartNet demonstrate that Owner3D consistently outperforms existing 3D stylization methods in target-region style fidelity and non-target appearance preservation, reducing appearance leakage by 86.4% and 89.9% compared with StyleSplat and LAENeRF, respectively.

</details>


### [36] [CoDS: Robust Collaborative Perception via Expert-driven Detection and BEV Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14085)
*Jinlong Wang,Yuang Jia,Junhong Lin,Nannan Li,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDS框架，通过协同可靠性图（CoRM）、语义专家混合（S-MoE）模块和双向任务互补交互（BTCI）来应对多源噪声，提升协作感知中特征融合质量，实现检测与BEV分割的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角感知受限于视野，多智能体信息交换虽能突破此限制，但存在姿态误差、通信延迟等多源噪声，影响特征融合质量，进而制约感知性能。因此需要一种鲁棒的协作感知框架来提升系统在复杂环境下的表现。

Method: 引入协同可靠性图（CoRM）量化特征质量分布；设计语义专家混合（S-MoE）模块以适应不一致的特征需求；采用双向任务互补交互（BTCI）机制，通过任务间信息反向注入提升特征鲁棒性。

Result: 在OPV2V和V2V4Real数据集上的实验表明，CoDS在检测与BEV分割任务上均优于现有基线方法，并在多源噪声下保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: CoDS框架有效缓解了多源噪声对协作感知的影响，通过多任务协同与特征自适应机制显著提升了感知系统的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Collaborative perception breaks through single-view limitations via multi-agent information exchange. However, multi-source noise such as pose errors and communication delays degrades fusion feature quality, constraining perception performance. Joint training of detection and BEV segmentation provides a natural remedy, where segmented road regions help constrain target distributions and detection bounding boxes help recover ambiguous segmentation boundaries. To this end, we propose a robust Collaborative perception framework with expert-driven Detection and bev Segmentation (CoDS). To address spatial inconsistency in fusion quality, we first introduce the Collaborative Reliability Map (CoRM) to explicitly quantify feature quality distribution. Based on CoRM, we design the Semantic Mixture-of-Experts (S-MoE) module to extract differentiated features for inconsistent feature demands. Finally, to further mitigate feature noise degradation, the Bidirectional Task Complementary Interaction (BTCI) refines task-aware features through bidirectional injection. Extensive experiments on OPV2V and V2V4Real datasets show that our CoDS surpasses existing baselines on both tasks and maintains stable robustness under multi-source noise. Code: https://github.com/JinlongW128/CoDS and https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenAIDriving/CoDS.

</details>


### [37] [Fixed-Budget Gaussian Volume Encoding with Structure-Aware Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14112)
*Michael R. Martin,Joseph Insley,Victor A. Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于固定资源预算的各向异性高斯基元编码方法，用于高效压缩科学模拟产生的标量场。通过分析局部场结构直接分配完整基元集，并在不进行密集化、修剪或数量调整的情况下直接优化基元以逼近原始数据。该方法显著减少编码时间（最高达51倍），并在单个桌面GPU上实现每分钟数百万高斯基元的快速编码。在多个数据集上，压缩比高达40,000倍，同时保持15.0–38.7 dB的高保真度（PSNR）。基元保留标量属性，支持无需重编码的后期可视化操作，如转移函数、着色、光照和视角变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟生成的标量场数据量巨大，远超存储、传输和加载能力；而原位压缩必须限制对模拟资源的占用。现有方法通常依赖迭代优化、密度调整或模型剪枝，效率低且难以控制资源使用。因此需要一种在有限资源下高效、可预测且支持灵活后处理的压缩方法。

Method: 将标量场表示为各向异性高斯基元，基于局部场结构（位置、方向、形状）进行一次性解析分配，随后直接在原始场上进行非迭代式精炼，不改变基元数量。通过设定预算控制编码时间和基元数量，结合迭代调度实现精细的时间-精度权衡。引入截断感知场评估加速计算。

Result: 在控制基准测试中，编码速度提升最高达51倍；140万高斯基元可在四分钟内压缩十亿体素数据，简化迭代精炼可在一分钟内完成。在五组数据集上，压缩比达2.2x至超过40,000x，PSNR保持在15.0–38.7 dB之间。预编码结构统计可识别出单次分配已接近性能上限的场景。单一紧凑模型支持所有后续可视化状态，无需重新编码。

Conclusion: 所提方法在严格资源约束下实现了高效、高质量的标量场压缩，兼具快速编码、极高压缩比与强大的后处理灵活性，适用于大规模科学数据的原位处理与交互式可视化。

Abstract: Scientific simulations often produce scalar volumes faster than they can be stored, transferred, and loaded, while in situ reduction must use only a limited share of simulation resources. This work encodes scalar fields as anisotropic Gaussian primitives under a fixed budget. The complete primitive set is allocated analytically from local field structure, including position, orientation, and shape, then refined directly against the scalar field without densification, pruning, or count changes. The selected budget determines encoded storage before refinement and, together with the iteration schedule, provides a controllable refinement-time budget. In a controlled benchmark, truncation-aware field evaluation reduces encoding time by up to 51x; 1.4 million Gaussians encode a billion-voxel volume in at most four minutes on one desktop GPU, with reduced-iteration refinement completing in under one minute. Across five datasets spanning 2.1 million to 1.1 billion evaluated voxels, compression-useful configurations achieve 15.0-38.7 dB PSNR at compression ratios from 2.2x to over 40,000x. Pre-encoding structure statistics characterize fields for which one-shot allocation yields limited gains from additional capacity. Because primitives retain scalar attributes rather than baked appearance, a single compact model serves every subsequent visualization state - supporting post-hoc transfer-function, colormap, lighting, and viewpoint changes without re-encoding.

</details>


### [38] [HiCo-GS: Hierarchical Context Aggregation and Geometric Consistency for Octree Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14136)
*Wei Zhang,Shengkai Yu,Shiqiang Gong,Qi Zhang,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiCo-GS提出一种高保真重建框架，解决城市级新视角合成中多层级锚点特征孤立导致的颜色漂移和过度平滑问题。通过跨层级上下文聚合（CLCA）实现层级间双向信息传递，利用八叉树的空间包含结构融合父-自-子上下文向量；同时引入深度-法向几何一致性（DNGC）正则化，通过渲染法向与深度推导法向的一致性损失抑制浮动物体。在真实与合成城市数据集上表现优异，尤其在复杂古建筑细节还原方面有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于八叉树的锚点高斯溅射方法存在跨层级特征孤立问题，导致建筑立面颜色漂移和纹理区域过度平滑，影响重建质量。

Method: 提出两个互补模块：1）跨层级上下文聚合（CLCA），利用八叉树空间结构将各层级上下文向量融合为父-自-子三元组，通过轻量级MLP与残差连接实现双向信息传递；2）深度-法向几何一致性（DNGC）正则化，通过加权一致性损失及渐进式边缘感知平滑损失，强化城市几何中的平面先验以减少浮动物体。

Result: 在Mill19、UrbanScene3D、MatrixCity及自建中国佛塔数据集（China-Pagoda）上均达到当前最优渲染质量，几何更清晰，尤其在密集雕刻、曲线屋檐和重复纹理等复杂细节上表现突出。

Conclusion: HiCo-GS通过引入跨层级上下文交互与几何一致性约束，有效缓解了传统方法中的颜色漂移与过平滑问题，在城市级场景的新视角合成中实现了更高保真度的重建效果。

Abstract: Octree-based anchor Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a scalable representation for city-scale novel view synthesis, where multi-level anchors adaptively capture scene content from coarse building structures to fine architectural details. However, we identify a fundamental limitation in existing methods: cross-level feature isolation, where each level's anchor features are optimized independently with no inter-level communication, causing color drift on building facades and over-smoothing in textured regions. We present HiCo-GS, a high-fidelity reconstruction framework with two complementary modules. Cross-Level Context Aggregation (CLCA) enables bidirectional hierarchical prior injection by leveraging the octree's spatial containment structure to aggregate per-level context vectors into parent-self-child triplets, fused via a lightweight MLP with residual connection. Coarse-level structural priors flow down to inform fine-level anchors, while fine-level detail statistics feed back to prevent over-smoothing, at negligible computational overhead. Depth-Normal Geometric Consistency (DNGC) regularization enforces agreement between rendered normals and depth-derived normals through an alpha-weighted consistency loss, complemented by edge-aware smoothness losses with progressive warmup that exploit the strong planar priors ubiquitous in urban geometry to suppress floating artifacts. We further introduce the China-Pagoda dataset comprising 8 ancient Chinese pagodas with over 1,200 images each, featuring dense ornamental carvings, curved multi-layer eaves, and repetitive fine-grained textures. Extensive experiments on Mill19, UrbanScene3D, MatrixCity, and China-Pagoda demonstrate that HiCo-GS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality and substantially cleaner geometry across real-world and synthetic urban benchmarks.Code: https://github.com/WZ-CS/HiCo-GS.

</details>


### [39] [SPARGen: Unifying Spatial Perception and Reasoning through Native Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14138)
*Jinsheng Quan,Jianhua Li,Siyi Xie,Xuanke Shi,Kewang Deng,Zukai Chen,Feifei Shao,Lei Yang,Quan Wang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARGen是一个统一的多模态框架，将3D重建、密集对应关系和空间推理任务统一为指令条件生成任务，通过序列化结构化和语言输出，实现图像对齐的密集几何场生成，从而在单一多模态生成模型中联合优化共享表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常分别处理空间感知与推理任务，使用特定任务架构或外部几何模块，限制了互补表征之间的知识迁移。

Method: SPARGen将3D重建、密集对应和空间推理统一为指令条件生成任务，通过序列化结构化与语言输出，生成图像对齐的密集几何场，实现空间监督下的共享表征联合优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，SPARGen在3D重建、对应关系和空间推理任务上均表现出色，证明其在单一多模态生成框架内具备跨异构空间任务的竞争力。

Conclusion: SPARGen成功实现了多空间任务的统一建模，提升了不同空间能力间的知识共享与协同优化，为视觉场景理解提供了高效、统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial perception and reasoning from visual observations require recovering geometric structure, establishing correspondences, and understanding spatial relations. Existing approaches typically address these capabilities separately using task-specific architectures or external geometric modules, limiting knowledge transfer among complementary representations of the same physical scene. We introduce SPARGen, a unified multimodal framework that casts 3D reconstruction, dense correspondence, and spatial reasoning as instruction-conditioned generation tasks. SPARGen serializes compact structured and linguistic outputs as token sequences while generating dense geometric fields in image-aligned forms, enabling spatial supervision to jointly shape shared representations within a native multimodal generative model. Experiments across benchmarks for 3D reconstruction, correspondence, and spatial reasoning show that SPARGen achieves competitive performance across heterogeneous spatial tasks within a single native multimodal generative framework.

</details>


### [40] [PISA: A Pseudo-Individual Source-Domain Feature Adaptation Framework for Test-Time Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14142)
*Ziyan He,Xiongtai Yang,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PISA提出一种无需源域数据的开放词汇目标检测测试时自适应方法，通过构建抗干扰特征提取器（CIFE）和特征对齐模块（FAM），利用图像扰动下的特征不变性来生成密集且具体的伪个体源域特征，从而在不依赖不可靠伪标签的情况下提升检测精度。该方法在VOC-C、COCO-C和LVIS-C等基准上显著优于现有方法，尤其在COCO-C上达到3.92%的AP@50%提升，实现当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源域无关的开放词汇目标检测测试时自适应方法依赖于重评分或伪标签进行自训练，但当初始预测质量差时会导致性能严重下降；同时传统源域估计方法仅恢复抽象稀疏表示，难以满足检测任务所需的密集具体特征需求。

Method: 提出PISA方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 噪声不变特征提取器（CIFE），利用CLIP视觉特征在不同扰动图像中的不变性以提取鲁棒特征；(2) 特征对齐模块（FAM）与多尺度对齐框架（BAA），将噪声不变特征转化为接近原始源域特征的伪个体源域特征，实现更有效的监督信号。整个方法可无缝集成到开放词汇视觉骨干网络中。

Result: 在VOC-C、COCO-C和LVIS-C三个带扰动的基准上验证，PISA显著提升了模型的定位精度与类别识别准确率；尤其在COCO-C上相比现有方法提升3.92%的AP@50%，达到当前最佳性能，且无需访问源域数据。

Conclusion: PISA通过引入基于特征不变性的伪源域特征生成机制，有效克服了传统方法对劣质伪标签的依赖，实现了在无源域数据条件下的高性能目标检测迁移，为开放词汇目标检测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection test-time adaptation (OVOD-TTA) aims to address the performance degradation that pre-trained base models suffer when encountering image-domain shifts. Existing source-free OVOD-TTA methods rely either on refined test-time information for re-scoring or on pseudo-labels for self-training, leading to significant accuracy degradation when initial predictions are poor. Meanwhile, most conventional source-domain estimation methods recover abstract, sparse representations suitable for the classification task, but fail to capture the dense, concrete features required for detection. To address these issues, we propose PISA, a novel source-free OVOD-TTA method that can be seamlessly integrated into open-vocabulary visual backbones. The core components of our method are the Corruption-Invariant Feature Extractor (CIFE), the Feature Alignment Module (FAM), and a multi-scale alignment framework (BAA). To capture detection-suitable features, we develop CIFE to exploit the invariance of CLIP's visual features across corrupted images, ensuring robustness against various corruptions. We further develop FAM and BAA for the pre-training and adaptation to transform the corruption-invariant features into pseudo-individual source-domain features that are close to the original source-domain features. In this way, dense and concrete pseudo-individual source-domain features are used for supervision instead of unreliable pseudo-label signals. Experiments on the corrupted VOC-C, COCO-C, and LVIS-C benchmarks across three base models demonstrate that PISA substantially improves both the localization precision and the category recognition accuracy of the original models. Notably, PISA achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring access to source-domain data, surpassing existing methods by 3.92% in AP@50% on COCO-C.

</details>


### [41] [Self-Supervised Visual On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14144)
*Yijiang Li,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Bingyang Wang,Ke Zhang,Zhenfei Yin,Di Fu,Philip Torr,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需特权信息的自监督视觉在线策略蒸馏方法S²VOPD，通过从学生模型中减去信息来构建教师-学生间的不对称性，从而生成有效的学习信号。该方法利用图像的不对称增强视图，在不依赖真实标注、奖励或更强教师模型的情况下，显著提升模型性能。在六个细粒度感知基准上，S²VOPD将Qwen3.5-4B准确率从70.7%提升至77.4%，超越所有开源模型及GPT-5.4，且仅用原始训练数据就恢复了96%由特权信息带来的性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉在线策略蒸馏依赖于教师-学生之间的信息不对称，通常通过更强的教师模型或特权监督实现。但当缺乏此类特权信息时，如何构建有效不对称性成为关键问题。本文旨在探索在无任何特权信息条件下，如何通过反向设计（即削弱学生而非强化教师）来生成有意义的学习信号。

Method: 提出S²VOPD方法，通过在学生模型输入端使用强增强视图，而教师模型基于原始图像进行输出，形成一种基于增强的不对称性。该方法在在线策略中直接蒸馏教师分布到学生分布，以实现自监督学习。系统研究了多种视觉增强策略，分析其强度、类型与任务一致性对性能的影响。

Result: 在六项细粒度感知任务中，S²VOPD显著提升模型表现：将Qwen3.5-4B准确率从70.7%提升至77.4%，优于所有对比的开源模型，甚至超过235B参数的Qwen3-VL和GPT-5.4。在相同训练数据下，恢复了96%由特权信息带来的性能增益。实验验证了不对称性、增强强度及任务一致性的重要性。

Conclusion: S²VOPD证明了在无特权信息条件下，通过从学生侧主动削弱信息可有效构建学习信号。该方法简洁高效，无需额外监督或更强教师模型，为自监督视觉学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation relies heavily on an informative teacher-student asymmetry, through either a larger, stronger teacher or privileged supervision, such as reference answers or ground-truth regions of interest. This raises a fundamental question: where can informative asymmetry come from when nothing privileged is available? We answer this by inverting where the asymmetry comes from. Rather than adding privileged information to the teacher, we subtract information from the student. This asymmetry creates the same effective learning signal for free as a teacher with access to information unavailable to the student, without ground-truth annotations, rewards, or a separate stronger teacher model. Building on this principle, we introduce Self-Supervised Visual On-Policy Distillation (S$^2$VOPD), a simple yet effective method that constructs on-policy learning signals from asymmetric augmented views. S$^2$VOPD distills the teacher's distribution conditioned on the original image on-policy into the student distribution conditioned on a strongly augmented view of the same image. We systematically explore a broad design space of visual augmentations and uncover that (1) asymmetry matters: all four augmentation families improve performance, while symmetric self-distillation degrades it; (2) strength matters: performance peaks at a moderate strength; and (3) the gap must remain task-consistent: augmentations that completely remove the question-relevant evidence can induce large but uninformative discrepancies. Across six fine-grained perception benchmarks, S$^2$VOPD improves Qwen3.5-4B from 70.7% to 77.4%, above all open-source models compared, up to Qwen3-VL at 235B, and surpasses GPT-5.4. While holding training data the same, it recovers 96% of the improvement achieved by methods with privileged information. Website is at https://williamium3000.github.io/s2vopd

</details>


### [42] [CSG-Mamba: A Convolutional Scoring Gating Vision State Space Network for Endoscopic Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14146)
*Yuliang Wang,Jiaqi Wu,Jiaye Song,Shuxia Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CSG-Mamba模型，通过在编码器-解码器结构的瓶颈层引入卷积评分门控模块（CSG），有效解决内窥镜图像中低对比度边界、黏膜纹理干扰和高光等问题，显著提升息肉分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉状态空间模型在处理内窥镜图像时，因将2D特征转换为1D序列而削弱局部几何连续性，导致不规则轮廓过度平滑，影响分割效果。因此需要一种能保持局部细节并增强长程建模能力的新方法。

Method: 基于VM-UNet架构设计对称U形编码器-解码器，核心创新在于在语义丰富的瓶颈层嵌入卷积评分门控（CSG）模块，利用点卷积与大核深度卷积生成局部空间评分图，并通过乘法门控重新校准状态空间特征，实现更精确的特征调制。

Result: 在Kvasir-SEG数据集上达到0.9220 Dice和15.87 HD95，CVC-ColonDB上达到0.7418 Dice和0.6570 mIoU，优于多数基线模型，在重叠率和召回率指标上表现优异，同时保持良好的边界定位能力。

Conclusion: CSG-Mamba通过引入局部感知的卷积评分门控机制，有效缓解了传统视觉状态空间模型在处理复杂内窥镜图像时的局部信息损失问题，显著提升了息肉分割的准确性和鲁棒性，为计算机辅助结肠镜检查提供了强有力的技术支持。

Abstract: Accurate polyp segmentation is critical for computer-aided colonoscopy, yet endoscopic images often contain low-contrast boundaries, mucosal texture interference, specular highlights, and device-dependent appearance shifts. Vision State Space Models (SSMs) provide efficient long-range modeling with linear complexity, but existing Vision Mamba segmentation models typically convert 2D features into 1D scanning sequences, which may weaken local geometric continuity and over-smooth irregular contours. We propose CSG-Mamba, a convolutional scoring gating Vision State Space network for endoscopic polyp segmentation. Built on a VM-UNet-style asymmetric U-shaped encoder-decoder, CSG-Mamba inserts a Convolutional Scoring Gating (CSG) module at the semantically rich bottleneck. CSG generates a local spatial score map through pointwise and large-kernel depthwise convolutions and recalibrates state-space features by multiplicative gating. Experiments with three random seeds show that CSG-Mamba achieves 0.9220 Dice and 15.87 HD95 on Kvasir-SEG, and 0.7418 Dice and 0.6570 mIoU on CVC-ColonDB, outperforming the baselines on most overlap and recall metrics while maintaining competitive boundary accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [SCVIB: Editable State-Conditioned Visual Instance Binding forMulti-Turn Personalized Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14148)
*Xiongtai Yang,Ziyan He,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可编辑的状态条件视觉实例绑定（SCVIB）设置，用于多轮定位任务，通过协议定义的状态事件决定最终目标。构建了包含1050对支持-查询样本和1500个跨五个视觉领域、三种难度级别和四类目标状态依赖的实验场景。直接序列无关推理仅达到60.13% Joint@0.5，表明正确解析最终引用并不等同于有效利用对应视觉证据进行查询定位。为此提出TT-VG模型，结合目标状态转移树（TSTT）与视觉证据接地适配（VEGA），前者编译可见交互为协议事件并执行于版本化目标状态，后者基于轨迹衍生的同实例对进行支持条件下的实例定位。TT-VG达70.27% Joint@0.5，且在匹配目标解析下比最强基线高出16.20分。在反向时序和回滚任务中提升最大，表明其能有效处理非最新或恢复的支持证据。结果确立了SCVIB作为可控基准测试平台，并揭示了利用已解析支持证据进行查询侧同实例定位是多轮个性化定位的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多轮视觉定位任务中难以有效利用历史支持证据，尤其在需要回溯或恢复非最新支持实例的情况下表现不佳。因此，亟需一个更精确的评估框架和新模型来解决状态依赖性推理与视觉证据的有效融合问题。

Method: 提出TT-VG模型，包含两部分：(1) 目标状态转移树（TSTT），将交互过程建模为协议定义的事件序列，维护版本化的目标状态并追踪状态演变；(2) 视觉证据接地适配（VEGA），基于轨迹生成的同实例对，实现支持条件下的视觉证据精准定位。

Result: TT-VG在SCVIB数据集上达到70.27% Joint@0.5，显著优于直接推理（60.13%）和现有最强方法。在Counter-Recency和Rollback任务中提升最为明显，说明模型能有效处理非最新或恢复的支持证据，验证了其在复杂状态依赖推理中的优势。

Conclusion: SCVIB提供了一个可控且多样化的多轮视觉定位基准测试平台，而TT-VG通过结合状态演化建模与支持条件下的视觉证据适配，成功解决了多轮个性化定位中支持证据的有效利用难题，凸显了状态推理与视觉接地协同的重要性。

Abstract: We introduce editable state-conditioned visual instance binding, a multi-turn localization setting in which several support-defined instances are introduced across turns and protocol-defined state events determine the final target. We instantiate this setting as SCVIB, comprising 1,050 manually verified support--query base pairs and 1,500 episodes spanning five visual domains, three difficulty levels, and four target-state dependency groups. Direct Seq-free inference reaches only 60.13\% Joint@0.5, indicating that resolving the final reference does not ensure effective use of the corresponding visual evidence for query-side localization. We address this gap with TT-VG (Transition-Tree Visual Grounding), which combines a Target-State Transition Tree (TSTT) with Visual Evidence Grounding Adaptation (VEGA). TSTT compiles the visible interaction into protocol-defined events, executes them over versioned target states, and resolves the final-query reference to the corresponding support evidence. Adapted on trajectory-derived same-instance pairs, VEGA performs support-conditioned grounding of the resolved instance using a Visual Evidence Package. TT-VG reaches 70.27\% Joint@0.5; under matched target resolution, VEGA exceeds the strongest comparison method by 16.20 points. Gains over direct inference are largest on Counter-Recency and Rollback, which require routing to non-latest or restored support evidence. Together, these results establish SCVIB as a controlled testbed and highlight the effective use of resolved support evidence for query-side same-instance localization as a central challenge in multi-turn personalized localization.

</details>


### [44] [Concept Guidance: Precise, Training-Free Latent Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14172)
*Nikolai Röhrich,Isabell Hans,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为概念引导（Concept Guidance, CoG）的新方法，用于解决文本到图像扩散模型在概念特定控制和局部一致性方面的两大缺陷。CoG通过量化网络各层对特定概念的影响，利用概念相关层的加权组合来指导去噪过程，实现无需额外训练、外部模型或提示工程的目标特定引导。该方法在PixArt-alpha、SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.1-dev等主流模型上均表现出显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准文本到图像扩散模型缺乏对特定概念（如美学质量）的连续、精准控制能力，且在生成文字或人类手部等需要高局部一致性的内容时表现不可靠。这限制了其实际应用价值。

Method: 提出概念间互信息的概念，发现不同网络层对特定概念的影响存在显著差异；基于此，设计CoG方法，通过量化各层的概念相关性，并在去噪过程中跳过不相关层，使用相关层的预测结果进行加权组合以实现精确引导。

Result: CoG在多个目标和主流模型（如PixArt-alpha、SD3、SD3.5、FLUX.1-dev）上均实现了性能提升，且无需额外训练、外部模型、梯度或提示工程即可直接使用。

Conclusion: CoG通过揭示网络中概念特定结构的局部化分布，提供了一种高效、通用且无需训练的精准引导机制，显著提升了扩散模型在复杂生成任务中的可控性和可靠性。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have two major drawbacks that severely limit their practical utility: (1) standard models lack an intrinsic mechanism for continuous, concept-specific guidance (e.g., for precisely controlling how aesthetically pleasing an image looks), and (2) they lack reliability for tasks requiring high local coherence (e.g., generating text or human hands). To tackle these issues, we introduce a novel notion of concept-wise mutual information and find large, concept-dependent differences between individual layers, demonstrating that the generation of specific structures is localized in distinct parts of the network. We exploit this insight by reinforcing the impact of concept-relevant layers in Concept Guidance (CoG), a precise, target-specific guidance method that works for models out-of-the-box without additional training, external models, gradients, or prompt engineering. CoG first quantifies each layer's concept-specific impact and then guides denoising using a weighted combination of predictions generated with concept-relevant layers skipped. We demonstrate performance increases across various targets and popular models like PixArt-alpha, SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.1-dev. Code is available at https://github.com/CompVis/concept_guidance

</details>


### [45] [LightTeaNet: A Weakly Supervised Lightweight CNN for Multi-Label Tea Leaf Disease Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14178)
*Naif Haider Chowdhury,Md Rahim,Syed Farhan Hasan,Murad Hasan,Prithwiraj Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为LightTeaNet的轻量级卷积神经网络，用于茶叶病害的弱监督多标签分类与病灶定位。该模型利用图像级标签训练，结合类激活映射（CAM）实现自动病灶定位，无需繁琐的边界框标注。通过引入深度可分离卷积和通道注意力机制，在参数效率和特征区分能力方面表现优异。实验结果显示，其精确率、召回率和F1分数分别达到0.9615、0.8772和0.9179，mAP@0.50为0.1810，展示了在无手动标注条件下的良好定位性能，验证了该模型在农业智能病害监测中的可解释性与资源高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统茶叶病害检测依赖人工，存在不一致、耗时耗力且依赖大量监控的问题。为提升检测效率与准确性，亟需一种无需精细标注的智能诊断方法。

Method: 提出轻量级CNN模型LightTeaNet，采用图像级标签进行弱监督训练，结合类激活映射（CAM）实现病灶自动定位；集成深度可分离卷积以提升参数效率，融合通道注意力机制增强特征判别能力。

Result: LightTeaNet在无任何手动标注的情况下，取得精确率0.9615、召回率0.8772、F1-score 0.9179，mAP@0.50达0.1810，表现出较强的分类与定位性能，验证了其在农业病害监测中的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: LightTeaNet是一种可解释性强、资源高效的智能病害监测框架，适用于实际农业场景中茶叶病害的自动化识别与定位，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Tea is known as an important crop in many parts of South and Southeast Asia, yet the production of tea is still hampered by the multiple diseases that decrease the quantity and quality. Traditional methods of inspection, which are manual, are not consistent, labor-intensive, and depend on extensive monitoring. This paper introduces a lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for weakly supervised multi-label classification and disease localization in tea leaves called LightTeaNet. LightTeaNet learns directly from image-level labels and employs Class Activation Mapping (CAM) to localize disease-affected regions automatically, unlike conventional object detection models such as YOLO, which require extensive bounding box annotations. For Parameter efficiency, the network integrates Depthwise Separable Convolutions, and for enhanced feature discrimination, it integrates Channel Attention. LightTeaNet has achieved a Precision of 0.9615, a Recall of 0.8772, and an F1-score of 0.9179, while it shows mAP@0.50=0.1810 without any manual annotations, which delivers a competitive localization performance in the experimental results. These results validate the model as an interpretable as well as a resource-efficient framework for intelligent disease monitoring in agriculture.

</details>


### [46] [RankT2I: A Submodular Framework for Discovering Interpretable and Diverse Semantics in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14226)
*Ritika Allada,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RankT2I 是一种无需训练、模型无关的框架，用于自动发现扩散模型和 FLUX 模型中可编辑的语义。它利用多模态视觉-语言模型生成候选语义，并通过子模优化选择相关、可编辑且多样化的语义集合，显著提升文本到图像编辑中语义发现的效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在文本到图像编辑中需手动指定可修改的语义，过程繁琐且依赖大量试错。为解决这一问题，需要一种自动化、高效且通用的语义发现方法。

Method: 首先使用多模态视觉-语言模型获取广泛候选语义；然后将语义发现建模为集合选择问题，采用子模函数目标来挑选相关、可编辑且多样化的语义。

Result: RankT2I 能够在多个视觉领域中高效识别大量可编辑语义，在性能上优于现有方法，显著减少用户手动探索成本。

Conclusion: RankT2I 为文本到图像编辑提供了一种有效、自动化且通用的语义发现框架，推动了图像编辑的智能化发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) models have revolutionized the field of image generation and editing. However, identifying semantics that a T2I model can successfully edit in an image continues to be a challenging task. Most existing approaches require users to manually specify semantics to modify a particular image, a time-consuming process that often involves extensive trial and error. In this paper, we present RankT2I, a novel, training-free, and model-agnostic framework that automates the discovery of editable semantics in diffusion and FLUX-based models. Given a visual domain, we first utilize a multimodal vision-language model to gather a broad set of candidate semantics. We then frame semantic discovery as a set selection problem and use a submodular objective to identify semantics that are relevant, editable, and diverse. Our method helps users efficiently identify a wide range of semantics for text-to-image editing models across several domains while outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [47] [AppleScab-LT: A Longitudinal Real-Field Apple Scab Dataset for Temporal Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14235)
*Aamir Hilal,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Neeraj Goel*

Main category: cs.CV

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
