I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [182] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE-Bench是一个基于模拟器的基准测试，包含144个跨六个物理领域的源-目标适应对，旨在评估自演化代理在环境变化下的适应能力。通过代码驱动的设计，代理需在有限尝试次数内，利用诊断沙盒反馈将源环境代码迭代调整为可运行的目标设计。实验对比了十种来自四种范式的自演化方法，结果显示性能仍远未饱和：例如，Reflexion + Qwen3-14B仅成功35.9%的配对，而GPT-5.5在静态子集上达到66.7%的成功率。结果表明，基于模拟器的反思比未经验证的自我修正更可靠，记忆锚定会限制代理灵活性，而广度优先搜索虽探索充分但难以收敛。即使明确告知物理变化，性能提升有限，说明核心瓶颈在于机制重构而非参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化代理评估通常在固定执行条件下进行，缺乏对环境变化后恢复能力的测试。本文旨在填补这一空白，构建一个能真实反映代理在动态环境中持续适应能力的基准测试。

Method: 提出PACE-Bench基准，包含144个源-目标环境对，每个对具有相同目标与接口但物理规则不同；代理需在有限尝试中通过诊断沙盒反馈，迭代修改源代码以适配目标环境；采用多种自演化方法进行比较分析。

Result: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B仅在35.9%的配对中成功；GPT-5.5在静态子集上达66.7%成功率；揭示物理变化也无法显著提升性能；表明机制重构是关键瓶颈，而非参数调优或信息获取。

Conclusion: 模拟器引导的反思优于无验证的自我修正；记忆锚定限制适应性；广泛搜索难以收敛；当前性能受限于机制重设计，而非参数推理。该基准推动了对自演化代理真实适应能力的研究。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [183] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wyvern 是一个用于自动生成有依据的多模态技术报告的多智能体框架，能够整合文本、图像和表格，并通过声明自动修订阶段确保内容的准确性。人类评估显示，其生成的图表在87%的情况下被认为比基线更具有信息量，报告在63%到100%的情况下被认为比其他方法更有用。自动评估表明，Wyvern在引用召回率上提升2.3倍，在引用精度上提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能驱动的创新时代中，知识增长速度加快，难以跟上；生成模型虽广泛用于内容合成，但缺乏信息基础性，因此需要一种能生成有依据、多模态技术报告的自动化框架。

Method: 提出多智能体框架 Wyvern，支持多模态输出（文本、图像、表格）并集成参考文献，引入声明自动修订机制以增强内容的可追溯性和真实性。

Result: 人类评估显示，Wyvern 生成的图表信息量优于基线87%的情况；其报告在多数情况下被评价为更实用（63%-100%）。自动评估显示，引用召回率提高2.3倍，引用精度提高1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern 有效解决了生成内容缺乏信息基础的问题，显著提升了技术报告的质量与实用性，是实现高质量、可信赖多模态内容生成的重要进展。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [184] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SheetCompass框架，通过图引导和记忆驱动的方法解决大语言模型在处理复杂电子表格时的推理难题。该框架显式建模表内与跨表结构关系，并利用记忆保持任务相关性信息，增强对多维布局和隐含关联的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将复杂电子表格结构扁平化为字符串，丢失了重要的内部边界和跨表语义，导致大语言模型无法利用人类专家自然使用的全局空间上下文进行推理。

Method: 设计图引导和记忆驱动的代理框架，显式建模工作簿内的结构关系及跨工作表的关联，结合记忆机制保留关键信息以支持复杂推理。

Result: 实验表明，SheetCompass在复杂电子表格推理任务中显著优于现有方法，能有效捕捉跨表依赖、列间细粒度关系和空间布局特征。

Conclusion: SheetCompass通过结构感知和记忆增强，实现了对真实世界电子表格更准确的自动化推理，为大语言模型在办公自动化中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [185] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种考虑领域转移的双编码器迁移学习框架，用于解决台湾地区短时颗粒物（PM2.5）预测中因本地数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性差异大而导致的建模挑战。通过在10个美国站点预训练源编码器，并在77个台湾站点上进行适应性评估，结果表明冻结源编码器的双编码器模型表现最佳（MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725），相比基线模型有约4%-7%的误差降低。消融实验显示，保留台湾特异性分支对性能影响最大；允许源编码器在目标监督下自适应调整可进一步提升性能（MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析揭示预测主要受近期PM2.5浓度及影响污染物输送与扩散的气象因素驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异的情况下，仅依赖本地数据训练的模型难以捕捉复杂的时序动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移。因此需要一种能有效融合源域知识并保留目标域特性的方法来提升短时PM2.5预测性能。

Method: 提出一种移位感知的双编码器迁移学习框架，其中源编码器在10个美国监测点的小时级数据上预训练，目标编码器则针对台湾地区的77个站点进行特定表示学习。采用时间顺序的训练-验证-测试划分策略，在不同配置下对比模型性能，包括冻结源编码器、允许源编码器微调等设置，并结合消融实验和SHAP分析验证各组件作用。

Result: 在所有基准模型中，冻结源编码器的双编码器模型表现最优（MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725），相较TL-v1和TL-v2分别降低约7.1%和4.1%的MSE；当允许源编码器自适应调整时，性能进一步提升至MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739。SHAP分析显示预测主要依赖近期PM2.5值及与污染物传输相关的气象变量。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特异性信息并允许迁移表示在目标监督下自适应调整的前提下，能够最有效地提升短时PM2.5预测性能。该框架为跨区域空气质量预测提供了有效的迁移学习范式。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [186] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Twin系统通过模拟与交互自动构建游戏世界模型，无需人工设计，能在几乎所有ARC-AGI-3关卡中恢复真实规则与目标，利用回放验证和反例修复机制，实现97.8%的通关率，并在88.3%的关卡中超越人类效率。系统在87.2%的通关关卡中提前推断出目标，其余通过搜索发现。基准测试显示，基础模型仅得7.8分，经Twin优化后达93.3分，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需为每个任务手工设计世界模型，成本高且难以泛化；而真实游戏规则与目标隐藏，亟需一种能从交互中自动推断规则与目标的通用机制，以支持持续学习。

Method: 提出Test-time World-model Inference (Twin)系统，基于模拟与交互构建可执行的世界模型，采用强归纳先验处理网格类游戏，通过回放验证确保模型预测与实际行为一致，利用不匹配作为反例进行模型修正，结合目标推理与搜索机制完成任务。

Result: Twin系统成功通关179/183关（97.8%），在158关中比人类更高效（88.3%），在156关中于无奖励情况下提前推断目标（87.2%），基础模型得分从7.8%提升至93.3%，清除23/25游戏。

Conclusion: 构建可用世界模型比预期简单，真正的挑战在于正确推断目标；Twin系统展示了通过交互与验证自动生成有效世界模型的可行性，为持续学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [187] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了道德偏好获取在人工智能系统中的应用，指出在特征选择、投票者抽样和问题表述三个关键环节中，即使微小的决策也会显著影响最终的道德偏好结果。通过在三种不同场景下的实证研究（共809名参与者），发现不同情境下道德相关特征不同，政治意识形态对部分特征偏好有显著影响且方向可能反转，问题表述方式可扩大或缩小意识形态差距达一个量级，并改变道德基础与判断之间的关联。因此，基于投票的对齐机制无法仅靠聚合实现公平透明，必须对整个道德偏好获取流程进行审计与公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在社会中做出越来越多涉及道德抉择的决策，如何确保其行为符合人类价值观成为关键挑战。道德偏好获取被广泛采用，即通过调查公众对假设性道德困境的选择来训练AI模型。然而，该过程中的关键设计决策（如特征选择、投票者选取、问题表述）常被视为技术细节而未被充分审视，但这些选择实际上具有强烈的规范性影响。因此，本文旨在揭示这些选择如何塑造最终的道德偏好，推动对道德偏好获取流程的透明化与责任追究。

Method: 本研究基于一项跨情境的实证研究，包含两个阶段，共809名参与者，覆盖三个应用场景：AI肾分配、模拟缺席员工的AI代理、以及生成式AI对已故人物的描绘。研究系统考察了道德偏好获取管道的三个核心阶段：1）特征选择——分析哪些因素被视为道德相关；2）投票者抽样——评估不同政治意识形态群体的偏好差异；3）问题表述——比较不同措辞对参与者反应的影响。采用定量分析方法，包括统计检验、效应量计算及回归分析，以识别各阶段对结果的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1）道德相关特征在不同应用场景中存在显著差异，表明特征框架不能跨领域直接迁移；2）约三分之一的特征偏好受政治意识形态影响，且某些情况下方向相反，说明投票者构成会显著改变整体偏好分布；3）问题表述方式可使意识形态差距扩大或缩小至一个量级，同时改变道德基础（如公平、忠诚、尊重权威等）与判断之间的关系。

Conclusion: 基于投票的道德对齐机制并不能自动实现公平或透明，因为其结果高度依赖于设计者在特征选择、投票者选取和问题表述上的非显性决策。为确保道德AI的可信性，必须将整个道德偏好获取流程纳入审计范围，并对外披露各阶段的设计依据与潜在偏见。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [188] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型应用中会话切换的方法论与理论特性，将手交定义为任务相关的上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，区分了精确恢复先前信息与保留目标分布的区别。在外生性条件下，预测等价性刻画了最粗粒度的确定性充分手交，并给出固定长度比特需求。分析分离了记忆约束、写入者和延续过程的影响，量化了在已知下游查询前进行写作的成本。提出三部分记录：精确存储决策与约束、使用任务合理的统计量处理重复证据、保留未被统计量覆盖的原始观测。高斯线性回归提供精确的有限维手交及有限比特扰动界；非参数回归则给出与记忆成本相关的平方预测误差上下界。该研究为决定手交必须保留的内容及其内存需求如何依赖于后续任务提供了理论与方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当任务因上下文长度限制、应用重启或由另一代理接手而需跨会话延续时，需决定从前一会话传递哪些信息。现有方法缺乏对何种信息应保留的系统性理论指导，导致资源浪费或性能下降。因此，亟需建立一套关于会话手交的信息保留准则与理论框架。

Method: 将手交建模为任务相关上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，引入外生性假设下的预测等价性概念以刻画最小充分手交；设计三部分记录结构，结合高斯线性与非参数回归方法，分别推导精确与渐近边界，量化记忆成本与预测误差的关系。

Result: 提出了可实现精确有限维手交的高斯线性回归方案，以及基于非参数回归的内存-预测误差上下界关系；揭示了记忆成本受写入时机、写入者与延续策略影响，且在未知下游任务时提前写入存在显著代价。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了会话手交的理论基础，明确了手交所需保留的信息类型及其内存开销的决定因素，为大语言模型应用中的跨会话任务延续提供了可计算、可优化的决策框架。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: L-FNO 是一种结合了 FNO 式协变量路径、Lorentzian 谱核以实现历史依赖激发，并采用基于似然的训练目标的随机神经算子，用于建模稀疏事件场景下的随机事件动态。在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实世界数据集（包括疾病暴发预测与半导体故障/缺陷检测）上，L-FNO 在事件似然、校准诊断和罕见事件检测方面均优于回归型和似然型神经算子基线模型。结果表明，结构化的谱记忆和基于似然的学习为神经算子模型提供了有效的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子通常作为回归式函数到函数的模型进行训练，而非条件强度估计器，难以适应稀疏事件场景中的不确定性，尤其是由外生协变量和内生事件动态共同引发的罕见、突发且自激励事件。因此需要一种能有效捕捉历史依赖性并具备良好概率建模能力的新型神经算子。

Method: 提出 Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO)，其包含：FNO风格的协变量路径，用于处理外生信息；Lorentzian谱核，用于建模历史事件对当前事件的激发效应；以及基于似然的训练目标，使模型能够准确估计事件发生的条件强度。

Result: 在八项合成点过程基准和三项真实世界数据集上，L-FNO 显著提升了事件发生似然、改善了校准性能，并在罕见事件检测任务中表现更优，优于多种回归型和似然型神经算子基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构化的谱记忆机制与基于似然的训练策略为神经算子在随机事件动态建模中提供了有效的归纳偏置，显著增强了其在稀疏和高不确定性环境下的建模能力。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [190] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，当前研究和应用中过度依赖少数代码基准测试（如SWE-bench和LiveCodeBench）来评估大模型的通用编程能力，但这些基准容易被优化，导致评估结果与真实能力之间存在“意义鸿沟”。通过一个基于Django的案例研究基准套件，研究发现模型在这些基准上的排名缺乏泛化性，跨任务迁移能力弱，且针对SWE-bench的优化对其他任务或LiveCodeBench几乎没有提升。作者呼吁采用差异化评估策略：前沿模型用整体评估，研究使用多任务套件，具体任务则结合人机协作；同时建议建立能力分类体系并持续维护基准，以确保评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究和产品常将少量代码基准测试得分视为模型具备广泛编程能力的证据，但这些基准易被针对性优化，导致评估结果无法反映真实通用能力，形成评估与实际能力之间的脱节。

Method: 构建一个基于Django的案例研究基准套件，对在SWE-bench轨迹上后训练的模型及微调于特定Django模态的模型进行评估，分析其在不同任务间的性能表现与迁移能力。

Result: 模型在SWE-bench上的优化未能有效提升在新任务或LiveCodeBench上的表现，跨任务迁移能力差，基准排名不具备泛化性，表明单一基准评估不足以反映模型的真实编程能力。

Conclusion: 仅依赖少数基准测试无法可靠评估模型的多样化能力，应推动差异化评估策略、建立能力分类体系，并实施持续性的基准维护，以支撑更可信的研究与工程决策。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [191] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究XGBoost的鲁棒性，发现其性能易受垂直异常值和杠杆点影响。通过探索基于M-、S-和τ-估计器的替代损失函数，提出两步法MM-XGBoost，在鲁棒性与预测精度之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost虽强大且高效，但标准损失函数对异常值敏感，需提升其在存在异常数据时的稳健性。

Method: 采用M-、S-和τ-估计器设计新的损失函数，并提出两步法MM-XGBoost以增强鲁棒性。

Result: MM-XGBoost在面对异常值时表现更稳定，同时保持较高预测精度，是鲁棒性与准确性的最优权衡。

Conclusion: MM-XGBoost是一种有效的改进方法，显著提升了XGBoost在非理想数据条件下的可靠性。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [192] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在架构差异较大的情况下，如何通过非显式的神经元语义对齐，将大型模型的能力迁移至小型接收模型。提出一种基于激活引导的跨尺度融合框架APM，通过构建任务相关的激活图，选择关键层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元进行剪枝，并以微小插值系数注入到接收模型中，从而实现高效知识迁移。在16个基准测试上，平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%，多项任务性能显著改善，证明了无需精确结构对齐即可实现有效跨尺度融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构模型融合方法多依赖于显式神经元级语义对齐，但跨尺度融合中因架构差异大，难以实现精确对齐。本文旨在探索在无显式对齐条件下，是否可通过激活引导的方式提取并注入强模型中的核心功能组件，实现有效能力迁移。

Method: 提出Activation-Prune-Merge（APM）框架：首先在捐赠模型上构建任务相关的激活图，识别重要层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元；随后剪枝捐赠模型至接收模型架构；最后使用微小插值系数将剪枝后的捐赠模型片段注入接收模型，实现知识迁移。

Result: 在16个基准测试中，整体平均准确率由55.5%提升至60.6%；RTE准确率从64.3%升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%升至79.2%。分析表明，激活引导的剪枝能提升可迁移片段质量，且小比例融合仍具有效性。

Conclusion: 跨尺度异构融合可在无需显式语义对齐的前提下成功实现，关键在于捐赠模型的功能组件需高度集中且经激活引导精准选择，为轻量化模型能力增强提供了新路径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [193] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了不确定性采样在主动学习中表现不佳的原因，是由于标签污染还是错误集中在难例区域导致的有害影响。通过对比基于置信度的不确定性采样与随机采样，在三种公开二元表格数据集上，分别在干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）和依赖难度的有界噪声条件下进行实验。结果显示，在干净标签下，不确定性采样显著提升了学习曲线的平衡准确率；但在存在依赖难度的噪声时，其优势下降幅度大于随机噪声，且这种影响因数据集而异。暴露匹配分析未发现结构化错误位置具有普遍惩罚效应。此外，在干净的MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样虽提升平衡准确率，却降低了平均精度和特定假阳性率下的真正率。因此，不确定性采样虽具标签效率，但其鲁棒性取决于数据集、预算、噪声结构及评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不确定性采样在主动学习中失效的根本原因：是由于获取了更多被污染的标签，还是因为错误集中在困难样本区域导致更严重的负面影响。

Method: 采用100组种子，九种噪声率（0至0.30），标注预算从20到120，使用正则化逻辑回归并每次预算调整后通过交叉验证重新选择参数。比较了边际不确定性采样与随机采样在三种噪声设置下的表现：干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）、以及依赖难度的有界噪声。引入暴露匹配的RCN控制以确保最终获得的标签污染程度一致，并扩展清洁标签实验至预算400。

Result: 在干净标签下，不确定性采样使学习曲线的标准化平衡准确率提升1.09至1.77个百分点。在依赖难度的噪声下，其优势下降程度超过随机噪声，尤其在乳腺癌数据集上于六种噪声率下更为明显，而在其他两个数据集上未在任何测试率下观察到类似现象。暴露匹配分析未发现结构性错误位置存在普遍额外惩罚。在干净的MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提高了平衡准确率，但降低了平均精度和固定假阳性率下的真正率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样在主动学习中具有标签效率，但其表现出的鲁棒性并非普适，而是高度依赖于具体数据集、标注预算、噪声结构及所使用的评估指标。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [194] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核风险敏感均值p次幂（KRP）准则的随机向量函数链接（KRPRVFL）模型，通过将标准最小二乘目标替换为KRP损失，有效降低噪声标签、异常值和不平衡数据的影响，提升模型稳定性和泛化能力；结合协同学习机制与核特征映射，实现高效、鲁棒且可扩展的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL模型对噪声标签、异常值和不平衡数据敏感，限制了其在真实场景中的应用效果，亟需提升模型的鲁棒性与适应性。

Method: 采用KRP准则替代传统最小二乘损失，引入协同学习机制，并利用核映射捕捉非线性关系，无需显式选择隐藏层，保持高效性与可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面均优于基线模型，展现出快速、可扩展且可靠的分类能力。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL模型成功融合了RVFL的高效性与KRP准则的鲁棒性，是一种适用于复杂噪声环境下的高性能分类解决方案。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [195] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA 是一种两阶段框架，通过监督对比学习在多样化物理过程中学习嵌入空间，再在该空间中使用标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。相比基线自编码器，ORCA 在对新物理信号的敏感度上显著提升，且异常样本具有可解释性：通过最大似然模板拟合可量化地将异常事件归因于已知物理过程，并准确恢复注入的信号产率，包括未参与嵌入训练的信号。此外，还能通过已知过程描述缺失模板中的信号特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决碰撞物理中通用事件级异常检测存在的两个问题：异常分数难以解释，且与能量尺度和对象多重性高度相关。

Method: 提出 ORCA 框架，第一阶段利用监督对比学习在多种物理过程中学习嵌入空间；第二阶段在该嵌入空间中运行自编码器以生成异常分数。结合最大似然模板拟合，实现异常事件的可解释性归因。

Result: 在与高亮度大型强子对撞机条件一致的模拟数据上，ORCA 显著提升了对新物理信号的灵敏度（广度与深度），并使异常样本具备可解释性。模板拟合能准确恢复注入信号产率，包括未训练过的信号，并揭示未知信号与已知过程的相似性。

Conclusion: ORCA 为对撞机上的基于异常检测的搜索提供了一条可解释路径，其嵌入空间携带更高维的物理信息，优于传统一维输出拟合，有助于提升后续统计分析能力。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [196] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Query-derived Erase Direction (QED)，通过引入与键正交的查询相关擦除方向，解决线性注意力中因状态共享导致的干扰问题，提升长序列下的检索性能，使可用上下文长度在S-NIAH-1上几乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如GDN-2）在长上下文场景下因状态共享导致读取干扰，且其擦除机制无法有效应对由查询衡量的干扰，影响检索精度。

Method: 提出QED方法，增加一个基于查询且与键正交的第二擦除方向，在快速权重视角下利用可编辑部分抵消旧状态内容，从而减少干扰。

Result: 该方法在所有超过训练窗口的长度上均提升了检索性能，并在S-NIAH-1任务上使可用上下文长度几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: QED通过引入查询引导的正交擦除方向，有效缓解了长序列中的状态干扰问题，显著增强了线性注意力模型在长上下文场景下的表现。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [197] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从2018年10月到2026年7月，AI模型从BERT等简单系统发展为能够解决复杂数学问题和编写软件的大型智能体。自2024年底以来，解决实际编码问题的能力每年提升近六倍，成本显著下降，如OpenAI的GPT 5.6 Luna在每百万令牌仅需1至6美元的情况下，即可媲美高端模型。目前顶尖性能由专用模型分担：Claude Opus 5在前端编程中领先，Claude Fable 5擅长代码库级任务，GPT 5.6 Sol则在终端操作中表现最佳。在小学数学测试中，Qwen 2.5基础方法解决了58道题，高级采样方法可达79道；置信度排序工具在前50个预测中准确识别出47个正确答案，对任务排序极具价值，所有研究资料均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升AI在复杂任务（如编程、数学解题）中的性能与效率，降低使用成本，并推动模型专业化分工，同时提高可解释性与透明度。

Method: 通过持续迭代大模型架构、优化采样策略、引入置信度评估机制，并结合专业领域细分训练，实现性能突破与成本控制。

Result: AI在编程与数学任务上的能力年均提升近六倍，成本大幅下降，多模型专业化分工形成，置信度排序工具有效提升任务排序准确性，研究成果全面公开。

Conclusion: AI模型在通用与专业任务上取得显著进展，性能与成本效益同步优化，未来将向更高效、透明、分工明确的方向发展。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [198] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-PRISM is a post-hoc interpretability method that maps attribution scores from EEG foundation models into physiologically relevant domains like frequency and source space using linear transformations and backpropagation. It achieves high spectral recovery and moderate spatial accuracy in simulations and real clinical data, enabling interpretable analysis of seizures and autism biomarkers without retraining the model.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current explainable AI methods for EEG focus on time-channel spaces, which do not align with clinical intuition; there's a need for a universal, non-invasive way to interpret foundation models in clinically meaningful domains like frequency and source space.

Method: EEG-PRISM uses invertible DFT for frequency mapping and an approximately invertible EEG generative model for source localization, combined with backpropagation to transfer attribution scores across domains.

Result: In simulation, it achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, it correctly identifies delta-theta activity and localizes seizure onset with 50% accuracy. In autism, it correctly identifies frontal/temporal delta-alpha biomarkers consistent with prior findings.

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM provides accurate, theory-backed interpretation of EEG foundation models in physiologically relevant domains, supporting both window-level event analysis and group-level biomarker discovery, thus advancing clinical interpretability.

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [199] [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702)
*Kiran Nair,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE提出一种基于注意力熵的不确定性调制代理梯度机制，用于Transformer架构的脉冲神经网络（SNN），通过动态调整训练阶段的代理梯度斜率来提升优化性能，同时保持推理阶段不变，实现能效与精度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SNN训练中，固定形状的代理梯度难以适应不同层和训练阶段，导致优化效率受限；因此需要一种自适应机制以提升训练效果。

Method: 利用归一化自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，并以此调节训练时的代理梯度斜率，不改变推理模型结构和部署成本。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100数据集上，SAGE相比固定代理梯度基线，准确率提升1-2%，且在多种仿真时间步下表现一致稳定。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的不确定性信号可作为轻量级训练信号，有效支持Transformer型SNN中自适应代理梯度学习，具有显著应用潜力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient alternative to conventional deep neural networks by exploiting sparse event-driven computation, but their training remains challenging because the non-differentiable spike function requires surrogate gradients whose fixed shape may be suboptimal across layers and training stages. In this work, we introduce SAGE, an uncertainty-modulated surrogate-gradient mechanism for Transformer-based SNNs. SAGE estimates block-level uncertainty from normalized self-attention entropy and uses this signal to adapt the surrogate-gradient slope during training while leaving the inference model unchanged. By modulating only the training-time surrogate parameter, the proposed method preserves the original architecture and deployment cost while improving optimization flexibility. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 demonstrate that SAGE achieves improved accuracy over fixed-surrogate baselines, with results up to 1-2\% consistent gains across multiple simulation time steps. These results highlight the potential of attention-derived uncertainty as a lightweight training signal for adaptive surrogate-gradient learning in transformer-based SNNs.

</details>


### [200] [Capacity-Dependent Effects of Data Selection for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13721)
*Cuong Dang,Hoang Anh Just,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在推理监督微调中，基于似然性的响应选择方法的有效性，发现其效果取决于模型容量和训练时长。实验表明，高似然数据对小模型能带来快速稳定的早期提升，而大模型在长时间训练下则从低似然数据中获益更多。分析揭示小模型难以吸收低似然监督，易陷入浅层或重复行为，而大模型能更好地向教师分布迁移。研究提出容量受限的蒸馏理论框架，阐明数据难度、数据跨度与学生模型容量的协同作用。结论是：推理微调中的数据选择应结合模型容量和计算预算，而非单一偏好高似然数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于似然性的响应选择方法认为更接近学生分布的响应能提供更有效的监督，但其有效性是否普遍依赖于模型容量和训练时间尚不明确。本研究旨在重新审视这一假设，并探究不同条件下数据选择的实际影响。

Method: 通过控制实验，在数学推理任务上使用1.5B至8B参数的学生模型，由更强的教师模型生成监督信号，系统评估高/低似然数据在不同模型规模和训练周期下的表现。结合学习动态分析与容量受限的蒸馏理论建模，解释现象背后的机制。

Result: 观察到明显的容量依赖性“快适配/慢收益”模式：小模型在早期受益于高似然数据，大模型在长期训练中从低似然数据中获得更好性能。小模型难以吸收低似然信息，易产生重复或浅层行为；大模型则能有效利用困难样本。理论分析进一步揭示数据难度、数据跨度与模型容量之间的相互制约关系。

Conclusion: 有效的推理微调数据选择不应仅依赖高似然偏好，而需综合考虑模型容量与可用计算资源。最优策略应随模型大小和训练时间动态调整。

Abstract: In reasoning supervised fine-tuning, candidate responses for the same instruction can differ substantially in how well they match the student's current distribution. Recent likelihood-based response selection methods suggest that responses closer to the student distribution provide more effective supervision, motivating the hypothesis that high-likelihood responses may generally be preferable for fine-tuning. In this paper, we revisit this intuition and show that the value of likelihood-based data selection depends critically on model capacity and training duration. Through controlled experiments on mathematical reasoning, using students ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters and supervision generated by stronger teacher models, we observe a clear \emph{capacity-dependent} ``{\color{SMALLCOLOR}\textbf{Fast-Fit}} / {\color{LARGECOLOR}\textbf{Slow-Gain}}'' pattern. High-likelihood data provides faster and more stable early improvements, especially for smaller models, but low-likelihood data becomes increasingly beneficial for larger models when training is allowed to continue longer. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze learning dynamics, showing that small models often fail to absorb low-likelihood supervision and instead fall into shallow or repetitive behaviors, while larger models are better able to move toward the teacher distribution under such data. We further provide a capacity-constrained theoretical view of distillation that clarifies how data difficulty, data span, and student capacity jointly govern transfer. Overall, our findings show that effective data selection for reasoning should be aware of model capacity and computing budget rather than based on a single universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.

</details>


### [201] [The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13756)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了在vLLM中使用CUTLASS与Triton实现的相同缩放INT8 GEMM内核是否可互换，发现尽管各模型在冷启动下能复现自身结果，但两者在端到端推理中无一序列达成比特级一致（0/8, 0/16, 0/64）。通过验证无溢出条件下INT32点积的精确性与顺序无关性，排除了累加器作为差异来源的可能性。进一步实验表明，在幂次尺度下输出完全一致，而在真实检查点尺度下差异仅达bfloat16精度间距，说明差异源于缩放应用和输出舍入环节。引入探针检查点后恢复了端到端比特一致性。跨实现的FP8 GEMM显示差异随缩减深度增加而加剧，而INT8差异保持在百万分之一量级且在64倍K范围内仅差一个间距。教师强制重播揭示翻转集中在小对数概率边缘，可预测翻转风险（ROC-AUC 0.94）。研究将公开预注册数据、逐层预测、内核选择证据及合规性检测流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证不同GPU内核实现的INT8 GEMM在实际大模型推理中是否真正可互换，揭示潜在数值不一致的根本原因，并建立可操作的兼容性验证机制。

Method: 固定模型、硬件、推理引擎等条件，仅更换INT8线性层内核（CUTLASS vs Triton），通过端到端推理对比输出一致性；利用幂次尺度验证理论正确性，分析真实尺度下的偏差来源；采用教师强制重播定位翻转位置；构建可重复的合规性检测流程。

Result: 在真实缩放参数下，两种INT8内核产生最多一个bfloat16间距的差异，根源为缩放应用与输出舍入；在幂次尺度下实现完全比特一致；跨实现的FP8 GEMM差异随计算深度增长；教师强制重播可高精度预测翻转风险；提出一套可落地的内核互换性验证方法。

Conclusion: 尽管两个INT8 GEMM内核在理论上应等价，但在实际部署中因缩放与舍入策略差异导致非零数值偏差，必须通过系统性验证流程确保内核互换性，本研究提出了可推广的合规性检查机制。

Abstract: Two GPU kernels implementing the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface are usually treated as interchangeable. We test that assumption: holding the checkpoint, prompts, hardware, inference engine, decoding, and quantization configuration fixed, we swap only the INT8 linear kernel (CUTLASS versus Triton) inside vLLM. At 1.7B each arm reproduces itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts, yet the arms agree on no sequence in any end-to-end comparison we ran (0/8, 0/16, and 0/64). What makes this more than a benchmark discrepancy is an integer alibi: for shared INT8 operands under a verified no-overflow bound, the INT32 dot product is exact and order-independent, so the accumulator cannot be the source of any difference. Feeding both kernels identical operands from every linear layer of Qwen3-1.7B and 8B (196 and 252 layers), we find bit-identical outputs under power-of-two scales, confirming a pinned prediction list 196/196 and 252/252 (pre-registered at 1.7B, pinned but not blind at 8B), and observed differences of at most one bfloat16 spacing under the checkpoints' real scales. This localizes the divergence to scale application and output rounding after the exact accumulator. Applied as a probe checkpoint, the same intervention restores end-to-end bitwise agreement (8/8 and 16/16 sequences). Cross-implementation FP8 GEMM shows a different signature: both the prevalence and the magnitude of differences grow with reduction depth, while the INT8 fraction stays at parts per million and within one spacing over a 64x range of K. Teacher-forced replay ties layers to tokens: flips concentrate at small logit margins, which predict flip risk with ROC-AUC 0.94 on 16,384 positions. We will release the pre-registration, per-layer predictions, manifests with kernel-selection evidence, and a conformance procedure that turns these controls into a concrete check for kernel interchangeability.

</details>


### [202] [CutClean: Neural Network Pruning for Privacy-Preserving Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13773)
*Leonardo Magliolo,Vito Paolo Pastore,Giuseppe Valenzise,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CutClean方法，通过在神经网络各层添加辅助线性隐私头来量化信息泄露，并逐步增加稀疏性以减少敏感属性的泄露，实验表明该方法能有效降低隐私信息流同时保持高稀疏性和分类准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在高风险应用中部署时存在隐私泄露问题，即使没有传统数据偏差，仍可能泄露敏感信息，因此需要一种隐私感知的剪枝方法来减少信息泄露。

Method: 在每个网络块中引入辅助线性隐私头以量化信息泄露，并通过逐步增加稀疏性来消除私有属性泄露，以最后一层隐私头的准确率为衡量标准。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，CutClean能有效减少隐私信息流，实现高稀疏性并保持分类目标的准确性。

Conclusion: CutClean是一种有效的隐私感知剪枝方法，可在不损害模型性能的前提下显著降低神经网络中的隐私泄露风险。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications with growing privacy leakage concerns. We show that this privacy leakage can occur even in the absence of representation imbalances that lead to traditional dataset biases. This poses significant privacy risks when deploying models that process sensitive attributes. In this context, we propose CutClean, a privacy-aware pruning method that allows to reduce privacy information flow through the network, while increasing its sparsity. Our approach employs auxiliary linear privacy heads placed at each network's block to quantify information leakage, and further applies increasing levels of sparsity to remove the private attribute leakage, measured in terms of the accuracy of the privacy head attached to the last block. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively minimizes private information flow while achieving high sparsity rates and preserving classification target accuracy.

</details>


### [203] [PPAPlace: Differentiable Cross-Stage Objectives for Chip Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13790)
*Ruogu Chen,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPAPlace提出了一种基于后全局布线阶段标签的时序驱动可微分代理模型，用于预测芯片的后布线性能、功耗和面积（PPA）。该模型采用双流架构，结合网表图注意力与布局网格空间卷积，通过端到端梯度反传优化宏单元位置。在五个未参与训练的ChiPBench测试电路上，PPAPlace相比层次化基线平均提升22% WNS和51% TNS，同时保持功耗和可布线性不变，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布局方法主要优化半周长线长（HPWL），但其与后布线时序指标（如WNS、TNS）相关性极低，导致AI布局工具性能反而劣于传统方法。此外，现有方法依赖预布线指标作为训练标签，但这些标签对最终时序的反映不准确，限制了布局优化效果。

Method: 提出PPAPlace，一种时序驱动的可微分代理模型。该模型利用后全局布线阶段的时序信息作为训练标签，通过双流结构（图注意力+空间卷积）建模宏观与标准单元布局，并生成可用于优化的WNS和TNS梯度。该梯度被用于两种方式：一是在分析式布局器中作为协同目标函数；二是在布局后通过投影梯度下降进行宏位置精调。

Result: 在五个未参与训练的ChiPBench测试电路上，PPAPlace-CoOpt和PPAPlace-Refine分别实现平均22%的WNS改善和51%的TNS改善，同时保持功耗和可布线性不变。模型无需针对新电路重新训练，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PPAPlace证明了使用后全局布线阶段的高保真标签训练可微分代理模型的有效性。该方法显著提升了布局对后布线时序的预测与优化能力，为下一代AI驱动的物理设计提供了新的范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Macro placement significantly affects a chip's post-route performance, power, and area (PPA). Most placement methods optimize half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) as the primary objective. However, recent benchmarking shows a near-zero correlation between HPWL and post-route timing metrics such as the worst negative slack (WNS) and total negative slack (TNS). As a result, all six evaluated artificial intelligence (AI) placers degraded PPA relative to the hierarchical baseline. Recent efforts have tried to train cross-stage predictors to close this gap. However, existing methods focus on macro-only representations and use pre-route metrics as training labels. A label fidelity study of ten circuits at four design flow stages reveals that HPWL and pre-route timing poorly reflect final post-route timing rankings. In contrast, post-global-routing achieves the best balance between final timing fidelity and label generation cost-effectiveness. Based on this finding, PPAPlace is a timing-driven differentiable surrogate predicting post-route PPA from macro and standard-cell placements. The surrogate is a dual-stream predictor that combines graph attention over the chip netlist with spatial convolution over the placement grid. It is trained on post-global-routing labels. The predicted WNS and TNS gradients flow end-to-end back to cell coordinates. PPAPlace exploits these gradients in two ways: as a co-objective injected into an analytical placer's optimization loop (PPAPlace-CoOpt), and as a post-placement refinement step that adjusts macro positions via projected gradient descent (PPAPlace-Refine). On five ChiPBench test circuits excluded from training, PPAPlace improves average WNS and TNS by 22\% and 51\% over the hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability, using the same predictor without test-circuit retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/ValleyC/PPAPlace.

</details>


### [204] [Recent Advances in Deep Learning-Based Drug-Target Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13797)
*Jafin Khan,Md Hossain Shuvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对近年来用于药物-靶标结合亲和力预测的机器学习方法进行了全面综述与比较分析，重点关注方法的优势、局限性和研究空白。文章回顾了多种深度学习方法，涵盖不同神经网络架构和表示策略，并分析了七个常用基准数据集及评估指标。结果显示，尽管多数方法在标准基准上表现良好，但其性能受数据集偏差和评估设置限制影响较大，且在冷启动场景下表现下降，表明泛化能力不足。当前方法存在数据不平衡、评估不标准化、现实应用有限以及冷启动问题等挑战。未来研究方向包括改进数据集设计、更稳健的评估方法、优化冷启动处理以及多模态表征融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测药物-靶标结合亲和力是药物发现中的关键挑战，尽管已有诸多进展，但仍面临泛化能力差、评估不一致、冷启动问题等难题，亟需系统性梳理现有方法并识别研究空白。

Method: 采用文献综述与对比分析方法，系统回顾近年来代表性深度学习模型，基于常见基准数据集和评估指标进行归纳分析，涵盖神经网络架构、特征表示策略，并对数据集特性与评估方式展开深入讨论。

Result: 多数方法在标准数据集上表现优异，但受数据集偏差影响，实际泛化能力有限；冷启动场景下性能显著下降；存在数据不平衡、评估不统一、真实应用场景适配性差等问题。

Conclusion: 当前方法虽有进展，但在可泛化性、评估严谨性和实际应用方面仍存在明显不足。未来应致力于构建更高质量的数据集、制定统一评估标准、提升冷启动应对能力，并探索多模态信息融合以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Computational approaches to drug discovery involve multiple sub-problems, and among them, drug-target binding affinity prediction plays an important role. Despite recent advances, accurately predicting binding affinity remains an open research area. The major objective of our paper is to perform a comprehensive review and comparative analysis of recent machine learning methods for drug-target binding affinity prediction, with a focus on identifying strengths, limitations, and research gaps. We review representative recent deep learning approaches that use common benchmark datasets and evaluation metrics, covering a range of neural network architectures and representation strategies. In addition, we analyze seven widely used benchmark datasets and commonly adopted evaluation metrics for drug-target binding affinity prediction. Our analysis indicates that although many methods report strong performance on standard benchmarks, their effectiveness is often influenced by dataset bias and limited evaluation settings. Furthermore, most methods exhibit reduced performance in cold-start scenarios, highlighting challenges in generalization. We identify several limitations of current approaches, including dataset imbalance, the lack of standardized evaluation, limited real-world applicability, and challenges in cold-start scenarios. We also discuss future research directions, including better dataset design, more robust evaluation methods, improved handling of cold-start problems, and the integration of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [205] [Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing with Online Requests: Event-Driven Transformer--DRL and Rolling-Horizon Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13799)
*Faezeh Ardali,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态多仓库车辆路径问题的事件驱动学习与基准测试框架，采用掩码MLP和Transformer策略通过行为克隆与近端策略优化训练。通过确定性可行性掩码防止无效分配，固定前缀/灵活后缀路由承诺保护已完成、进行中和近期决策，并分别衡量车辆重新分配与重排序。所学策略与动态插入启发式算法及时间限制滚动时域优化方法进行比较。在20个场景的基准测试中，所有方法均完成所有请求且无无效动作，但最近可行法在平均目标值上表现最佳，优于学习策略在路线质量、等待时间、稳定性、完工时间和运行时间方面。五次独立训练中，PPO对MLP影响较小，但平均提升Transformer性能，尽管种子变异性较大。在通用协议下，最近可行法实现最低综合目标与路线扰动；滚动时域优化则在等待时间与完工时间上最优，但计算成本显著更高。学习策略保持毫秒级决策速度，并可在不重新训练情况下迁移至最多80个请求的实例，但未超越最强启发式算法。单一方法无法在路线效率、服务响应、稳定性和在线计算中全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态多仓库车辆路径问题中请求逐步揭示与车辆状态演变带来的复杂决策挑战，探索学习型策略与传统启发式方法的性能边界，以评估智能决策在实际应用中的有效性与适用性。

Method: 采用掩码MLP与Transformer作为策略网络，通过行为克隆与近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练；引入确定性可行性掩码避免无效车辆-请求分配；使用固定前缀/灵活后缀路由承诺机制，保护已完成、活跃及近期决策；在20个场景下对比学习策略与动态插入启发式、时间受限滚动时域优化方法。

Result: 所有方法均能完成全部请求且无无效动作；最近可行法在平均目标值、路线质量、等待时间、稳定性、完工时间和运行时间上表现最优；PPO对Transformer有平均提升作用，但存在较大种子变异性；学习策略具备毫秒级决策能力并可跨规模迁移，但未能超越最强启发式方法；滚动时域优化虽在等待时间与完工时间上表现优异，但计算开销大；无单一方法在所有指标上均最优。

Conclusion: 尽管学习策略展现出良好的泛化能力与实时决策优势，但在当前设定下仍无法全面超越经典启发式方法。未来工作需结合学习与优化方法，以实现更高效、鲁棒与适应性强的动态路径规划系统。

Abstract: This paper presents an event-driven learning and benchmarking framework for the Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problem with progressively revealed requests and evolving vehicle states. Masked MLP and Transformer policies are trained through behavior cloning and proximal policy optimization. Deterministic feasibility masking prevents invalid vehicle--request assignments, while fixed-prefix/flexible-suffix route commitments protect completed, active, and near-term decisions and separately measure vehicle reassignment and resequencing. The learned policies are compared with dynamic insertion heuristics and time-limited rolling-horizon optimization. In a 20-scenario policy benchmark, all methods completed every request without invalid actions, but nearest feasible achieved the lowest mean objective and outperformed the learned policies in routing quality, waiting time, stability, makespan, and runtime. Across five independent training runs, PPO had little average effect on the MLP and improved the Transformer on average, although with greater seed variability. Under the common protocol, nearest feasible achieved the lowest combined objective and route disruption, whereas rolling horizon achieved the lowest waiting times and makespan at substantially higher computational cost. The learned policies retained millisecond-level decisions and transferred to instances with up to 80 requests without retraining, but did not outperform the strongest heuristic. No single method was best across routing efficiency, service responsiveness, stability, and online computation.

</details>


### [206] [Stochastic Control Policies for Robust Molecular Transition Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13800)
*Jingqian Liu,Yu-Hsiang Wang,Yanru Qu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将基于滚动的控制方法重新表述为学习路径空间提议分布，并通过设计随机性放置来提升探索和优化的鲁棒性。提出了两种随机策略：FS-TPS直接参数化状态相关的高斯分布作为控制策略输出；LaS-TPS则采样一个紧凑的潜在控制变量并解码为具有原子间相关性的力变化。在三种不同大小的生物分子系统（丙氨酸二肽、Chignolin、BBL蛋白）上进行多种子实验，结果表明随机策略在提高过渡成功率和路径质量的同时，显著降低了对随机初始化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于分子动力学的过渡路径采样方法在生成稀有事件轨迹时存在不稳定性和强依赖初始条件的问题，尤其是基于滚动的控制方法。为了提升方法的鲁棒性和生成路径的物理合理性，需要改进其随机性设计以增强探索能力。

Method: 将滚动式控制方法建模为学习路径空间的提议分布，提出两种新的随机策略：FS-TPS（基于状态依赖的高斯分布）和LaS-TPS（通过潜在变量生成结构化的跨原子相关力变化），并通过多种子实验验证其性能。

Result: 在三个不同规模的生物分子系统中，所提出的随机策略在过渡成功率和路径质量方面均优于确定性基线方法，且显著降低对初始随机种子的依赖，表现出更强的稳定性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将控制策略建模为路径空间的随机提议分布，引入合理的随机性设计可有效提升过渡路径采样方法的稳定性与鲁棒性，为高效生成物理合理且可靠的稀有事件轨迹提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transition path sampling (TPS) aims to efficiently generate rare molecular transition trajectories between metastable states and is essential for understanding biomolecular mechanisms. Beyond traditional molecular dynamics (MD)-based sampling, machine learning has become central to state-of-the-art TPS. One major class of methods learns control forces during explicit MD rollouts. By preserving the underlying molecular dynamics, these methods tend to produce more physically plausible trajectories than endpoint-conditioned generators that construct paths directly. However, rollout-based control methods have been reported to exhibit unstable and strongly seed-dependent performance. We recast rollout-based control as learning a path-space proposal distribution and investigate stochasticity placement as a design choice for improving exploration and optimization robustness. We develop two stochastic policies: FS-TPS, which directly parameterizes a state-dependent Gaussian distribution over the control policy output, and LaS-TPS, which samples a compact latent control variable and decodes it into structured, cross-atom-correlated force variation. We conduct extensive multi-seed experiments on three biomolecular systems of increasing size: alanine dipeptide, chignolin, and BBL, a fast-folding protein. Stochastic policies consistently improve transition success and path quality over deterministic-policy baselines while substantially reducing sensitivity to random initialization.

</details>


### [207] [HI-MeshGraphNets: Efficient and Accurate Mesh-based Physics Learning with Hierarchical Multi-scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13827)
*SiHun Lee,Dong-Hyuk Park,Taesoo Bang,Seung-Hoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HI-MGN，一种基于多尺度的MeshGraphNets扩展，通过层次化图粗化和学习的插值网络实现高效长距离通信，显著提升大网格物理代理模型的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面消息传递在大规模高保真度网格上效率低下，因信息每层仅传播一跳，需深层网络处理长程交互，导致计算成本高、内存占用大及过平滑风险。

Method: 采用最远点采样与Voronoi划分进行图粗化，构建层次化多尺度处理器；通过粗图上的消息传递实现长距离信息传播，并利用学习的图插值网络重建细粒度特征。

Result: 在三个结构与流体基准测试中，相比MeshGraphNets和Bi-Stride多尺度GNN，HI-MGN在准确率、训练时间与峰值内存使用方面均表现更优，验证了拓扑感知的层次化消息传递与学习的粗到细插值的有效性。

Conclusion: HI-MGN提供了一种有效且实用的可扩展网格物理代理建模框架，通过层次化消息传递与自适应插值实现了高效长距离通信。

Abstract: Machine-learned physical surrogate models have become promising alternatives to mesh-based numerical solvers. Among them, graph neural networks (GNNs) are well suited for representing simulation meshes and learning nodal state evolution through message passing. However, conventional flat message passing becomes inefficient on large, high-fidelity meshes because information propagates only one hop per layer, requiring deep processors for long-range interactions and increasing computational cost, memory usage, and the risk of over-smoothing.
  To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Interpolating MeshGraphNets (HI-MGN), a multiscale extension of MeshGraphNets for efficient long-range communication on unstructured meshes. HI-MGN replaces the flat processor with a hierarchical multiscale processor that coarsens graphs using farthest-point sampling and Voronoi partitioning while preserving the original mesh topology. Message passing on coarse graphs enables information to travel over larger geometric distances with fewer layers, and a learned graph interpolation network reconstructs fine-resolution features.
  Across three structural and fluid benchmarks, HI-MGN achieves improved accuracy compared with MeshGraphNets and the Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN while reducing training time and peak memory usage. The results show that topology-aware hierarchical message passing and learned coarse-to-fine interpolation provide an effective and practical framework for scalable mesh-based physics surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [208] [Federated Prompt Learning: A Unified Framework, Empirical Analysis, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13844)
*Qinglin Yang,Chen Qiu,Hongyuan Zhang,Pengdeng Li,Yuan Liu,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文全面调查了联邦提示学习（FPL），系统梳理了其在大语言模型（LLM）训练与推理中融合联邦学习范式的研究进展，涵盖动机、方法、性能权衡及安全挑战，并提出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在训练与部署中面临的高计算成本、数据集中化和隐私问题，通过联邦学习实现无需共享原始数据的协作训练，保护隐私并提升可扩展性。

Method: 对现有联邦提示学习方法进行系统分析，覆盖预训练、微调到实际应用的全生命周期，评估其在性能、通信效率、计算开销、可扩展性、个性化及异构性处理等方面的权衡，并讨论安全、隐私与鲁棒性问题及防御机制。

Result: 总结了FPL在不同场景下的技术特点与性能表现，揭示了当前方法在通信效率与模型个性化之间的平衡难题，识别出安全与隐私漏洞，提出了若干关键挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 联邦提示学习为隐私保护的大语言模型训练提供了有效路径，但仍面临诸多挑战，需在算法设计、系统架构与安全保障方面进一步探索，以推动其在真实场景中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become core components of cloud-based intelligent services in academia and industry, yet their training and deployment are hindered by high computational costs, data centralization, and privacy concerns. Federated learning (FL) offers a decentralized training paradigm that enables clients to collaboratively train a learning model without sharing raw data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving LLM training and reasoning. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of federated prompt learning (FPL) to review recent advances in integrating the federated learning paradigm and large language models, answering the following research questions: RQ1: The fundamental motivations, characteristics, and enabling technologies of FPL, and how it differs from conventional FL and full-model federated fine-tuning; RQ2: The trade-offs FPL approaches exhibit in performance, communication efficiency, computational overhead, scalability, personalization, and heterogeneity handling; RQ3: The remaining security, privacy, robustness, and system challenges, along with key future research directions. To this end, we systematically examine existing FPL methods across the full model lifecycle: pre-training, fine-tuning, and practical applications, while discussing security, privacy, and robustness issues and summarizing existing defense mechanisms. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future directions, aiming to help readers understand how the insights drive research in FPL.

</details>


### [209] [Geometric Filtering of LLM-Generated Samples for Few-Shot Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13866)
*Benjamín Schindler,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何一致性的过滤框架，通过句子嵌入空间中的欧氏距离筛选大语言模型生成的文本分类数据，仅保留与真实类别样本几何上一致的样本，并使用软加权机制将过滤得分转化为训练权重。在13个数据集、5种分类器、10种增强方法和超过6,700种配置下评估，该方法相比SMOTE提升2.61个百分点（p<0.0001，Cohen's d=0.95，88.9%胜率）。该方法可直接推广至命名实体识别任务，提升9.26个百分点且胜率100%，对5个来自4家供应商的大语言模型均表现出鲁棒性。关键发现为：最简单的基于距离的过滤器始终优于复杂的多标准替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的合成训练数据质量不一，部分样本位于正确类别区域，部分则处于边缘或跨类别区域，导致分类性能下降。因此需要一种有效方法筛选高质量样本以提升模型表现。

Method: 在句子嵌入空间中计算每个大语言模型生成样本与真实类别样本的欧氏距离，仅保留几何上一致的样本；采用软加权机制将过滤得分转换为训练样本权重，用于分类器训练。

Result: 在13个数据集、5种分类器、10种增强方法及6,700+配置下，相比SMOTE平均提升2.61个百分点（显著性p<0.0001，效应量d=0.95，88.9%胜率）；在命名实体识别任务中提升9.26个百分点，胜率100%；对5个不同来源的大语言模型均表现稳健。

Conclusion: 基于距离的简单几何过滤方法在生成数据筛选中优于复杂多准则方法，具有高有效性、强泛化性和良好鲁棒性，适用于多种自然语言处理任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate synthetic training data for text classification, but the quality of generated samples is heterogeneous: some fall in correct class regions of the embedding space while others land in peripheral or cross-class zones. We propose a geometric filtering framework that evaluates each LLM-generated sample by its Euclidean distance to real class examples in a sentence embedding space, selecting only geometrically consistent candidates. A soft weighting mechanism transforms filter scores into sample weights for classifier training. Evaluated across 13 datasets, 5 classifiers, 10 augmentation methods, and over 6,700 configurations, our method achieves +2.61 percentage points (pp) over SMOTE ($p<0.0001$, Cohen's $d=0.95$, 88.9% win rate). The approach generalizes to named entity recognition (+9.26pp, 100% win rate) without filter modification, and is robust across 5 LLMs from 4 providers. A key finding is that the simplest distance-based filter consistently outperforms complex multi-criteria alternatives.

</details>


### [210] [Variation Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13882)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为变异性布朗核梯子（VBKL）的路径原子函数空间框架，通过分离非线性递归字典构建与线性变异性叠加，实现了深度模型在复杂度控制下的性能提升。该方法基于布朗再生核希尔伯特空间中的单位球形轮廓递归构造，利用符号测度变异性包络形成完整函数空间，并在局部非退化条件下证明了霍尔德正则性、紧致性、可达性及随深度严格增长的特性。针对有限支撑架构，通过布朗二次混沌和VC熵推导出径向基函数与泛化界。进一步通过双阶段近似实现 $M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}$ 的误差界和 $\sqrt{A/2}$ 的精确插值常数，且每评估最多仅需 $2M$ 个外层基函数贡献。实验验证了其逼近机制并展现出良好的小样本精度-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对深度有益性的论述依赖于对表示复杂度的定义，而当前方法缺乏对非线性递归结构与变异性叠加之间关系的清晰建模。本文旨在建立一个能明确分离这两者的函数空间框架，以更精确地分析深度带来的优势，并在理论上保障其正则性、紧凑性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出变异性布朗核梯子（VBKL），采用路径原子形式，从线性投影出发，递归地利用布朗再生核希尔伯特空间中的单位球形轮廓构建字典；最终函数空间为该字典的符号测度变异性包络。通过识别递归字典为布朗拉回RKHS球的并集，结合局部非退化条件，分析其函数空间性质。同时引入双阶段近似：离散化外测度与选定的布朗轮廓，从而获得具体的逼近误差界与计算效率指标。

Result: 建立了变异性控制下的霍尔德正则性、紧致性与可达性；在局部非退化条件下，函数表达能力随深度严格增长；对于有限支撑架构，获得了基于布朗二次混沌和VC熵的Rademacher与泛化界；双阶段近似实现 $M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}$ 误差界、$\sqrt{A/2}$ 插值常数，且每评估仅需至多 $2M$ 个外层基函数参与；实验显示在小数据场景下具有优异的精度-复杂度平衡。

Conclusion: VBKL提供了一个理论严谨且可解释的深度函数空间框架，能够有效分离非线性递归与线性变异性，揭示了深度在可控复杂度下的增益机制。其理论保证与实验表现共同支持深度结构在小样本学习中的优越性，为深度模型的设计与分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Claims about the benefit of depth depend on the complexity assigned to a representation. We introduce the \emph{Variation Brownian Kernel Ladder} (VBKL), a path-atomic function-space framework that separates nonlinear recursive dictionary construction from linear variation superposition. Starting from linear projections, each atom recursively composes unit-ball profiles from the Brownian reproducing kernel Hilbert space; the full VBKL space is then the signed-measure variation hull of the completed dictionary. We identify each recursive dictionary as a union of Brownian pullback RKHS balls and establish variation-controlled Hölder regularity, compactness and attainment, and strict growth with depth under a local non-degeneracy condition whose trace lies in the support of the input measure. For associated finite lower-support architectures, we derive Rademacher and generalization bounds through Brownian quadratic chaos, signed threshold traces, and VC entropy. We also construct two-stage approximants by discretizing the outer measure and the selected outer Brownian profiles, obtaining an $M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}$ error bound, a sharp interpolation constant $\sqrt{A/2}$, and at most $2M$ active outer-profile basis contributions per evaluation. Controlled experiments illustrate the approximation mechanisms and indicate a favorable limited-data accuracy--complexity trade-off.

</details>


### [211] [Fashion Outfit Generation via Unified Sequential Composition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13888)
*Kaicheng Pang,Xingxing Zou,Ruohan Xu,Waikeung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将时尚搭配生成任务形式化为受限集合生成（CEG），提出统一序列组合模型（USCM）联合建模整体搭配兼容性与潜在组合意图，并结合隐空间扩展蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS）实现高效物品检索，平衡局部美学协调与全局结构均衡。在Polyvore、iFashion和PolyvoreU数据集上的实验表明，该框架在人类偏好评估、自动美学指标和结构有效性方面均达到当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚搭配生成面临美学兼容性非单调且隐含、组合空间指数级膨胀等挑战，传统方法难以兼顾局部美感与整体结构平衡。

Method: 将任务建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程，提出USCM联合建模集合级兼容性与潜在组合意图，结合LE-MCTS进行隐空间引导的物品检索以优化生成过程。

Result: 在Polyvore、iFashion和PolyvoreU数据集上，所提框架在人类偏好评分、自动化美学指标和结构有效性方面均优于现有方法，展现出卓越的生成性能与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将时尚搭配生成建模为受限集合生成问题，并引入联合建模与智能搜索机制，本研究有效解决了大规模时尚物品组合中的美学一致性与结构合理性难题，为自动时尚设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: The task of synthesizing stylistically coherent fashion outfits from massive item libraries, known as fashion outfit generation, remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the non-monotonic and implicit nature of aesthetic compatibility, coupled with the exponentially large combinatorial search space. In this paper, we formalize this task as Constrained Ensemble Generation (CEG) and model it as a finite-horizon deterministic Markov Decision Process. To address CEG in fashion, we propose the Unified Sequential Composition Model (USCM), which jointly models set-level compatibility and latent composition intents. Guided by USCM's learned priors, a Latent Expansion Monte Carlo Tree Search (LE-MCTS) mechanism is proposed to handle item retrieval during composition, balancing local aesthetic synergy with global structural balance. Extensive experiments on the Polyvore Outfits dataset, along with zero-shot evaluations on the iFashion and PolyvoreU datasets, demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance across independent human preference evaluations, automated aesthetic proxies, and structural validity metrics for constrained fashion outfit generation.

</details>


### [212] [MedMix: Specialization-Consistent Federated Sparse MoEs under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13911)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Dong Min Kim,Jaehong Yoon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MedMix提出一种用于联邦多模态稀疏专家模型（sparse MoE）的语义对齐框架，以应对客户端和样本级的模态异质性问题。通过模态上下文感知路由与共识引导的路由对齐机制，在客户端层面实现基于模态身份、位置和不完整性上下文的专家选择；在跨客户端层面，利用服务器端共识锚点对齐本地路由分布，并结合客户端自适应专家聚合，匹配并聚合功能相似的专家。实验表明，MedMix在多种模态异质性和不完整性设置下均取得最优平均F1分数，尤其在严重异质性条件下表现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦多模态医疗AI面临客户端和样本级别的模态异质性：客户端可能系统性缺失某些模态类型，而同一客户端内的记录也可能包含不同的部分模态组合。现有的稀疏MoE架构虽能实现模态自适应计算，但在联邦学习中因跨客户端模态异质性导致本地路由策略分化，使专家专业化方向不一致，标准聚合方式会破坏其学习到的专家专长，影响模型性能。

Method: MedMix引入三重机制：1）客户端侧采用模态上下文感知路由，依据每个标记的模态身份、位置及不完整上下文进行专家选择；2）跨客户端使用共识引导路由对齐，构建共享模态模式的服务器端共识锚点，以对齐各客户端的路由分布；3）客户端自适应专家聚合，基于客户端特定的模态模式原型匹配并聚合功能相似的专家，避免因模态配置差异导致的错误合并。

Result: 在真实世界多模态医疗数据集上的实验结果表明，MedMix在不同模态异质性和不完整性设置下均实现了最优的平均F1分数，尤其在严重异质性条件下优势明显，验证了其在处理复杂现实场景中的鲁棒性与有效性。

Conclusion: MedMix通过语义对齐机制有效缓解了联邦多模态稀疏MoE在跨客户端模态异质性下的路由分歧与专家专化错位问题，显著提升了模型在不完整和异构多模态数据上的性能，为联邦多模态医疗人工智能提供了一种可靠且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated multimodal medical AI faces modality heterogeneity at both the client and sample levels: clients may systematically lack access to specific modality types, while individual records within the same client may contain different partial modality subsets. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are a promising remedy for modality-adaptive computation, but their use in federated learning is fragile under cross-client modality heterogeneity, where locally learned routing policies can diverge across clients and drive experts toward incompatible specializations. Different clients may assign the same observed modality configuration to different experts, or train similarly indexed experts on different missing-modality configurations, causing standard aggregation to misalign or overwrite the expert specialization that sparse MoEs are intended to learn. To address this challenge, we propose MedMix, a semantic-alignment framework for federated multimodal sparse MoEs that coordinates cross-client routing and expert specialization using modality context. At the client side, MedMix uses modality-context-aware routing to guide expert selection using each token's modality identity, position, and incompleteness context. Across clients, it uses consensus-guided routing alignment to construct server-side consensus anchors for shared modality patterns and align local routing distributions across clients. Complementing these routing mechanisms, client-adaptive expert aggregation leverages client-specific modality-pattern prototypes to match and aggregate functionally similar experts across clients. Experiments on real-world multimodal medical datasets show that MedMix achieves the best average F1 across diverse modality heterogeneity and modality incompleteness settings, with especially clear gains under severe heterogeneity.

</details>


### [213] [Hybrid Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Privacy-Aware Federated Biosignal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13914)
*Chun-Hua Lin,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Kuo-Chung Peng,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chi-Sheng Chen,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,En-Jui Kuo,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种量子启发的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（HQKAN），在联邦学习框架下用于心电图（ECG）分类，相较于传统多层感知机（MLP），HQKAN在MIT-BIH和INCART数据集上均表现出更优的性能，同时显著降低参数量与通信开销，在处理小样本、标签不平衡和非独立同分布数据方面更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG数据属于敏感生物医学信息，限制了医院和可穿戴设备间原始信号的共享；联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但面临客户端样本少、标签不平衡及数据分布异质等挑战，亟需高效且鲁棒的模型以应对这些约束。

Method: 采用混合量子启发的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（HQKAN）与多层感知机（MLP）对比，在联邦平均（FedAvg）框架下进行五类心律失常分类（MIT-BIH）和三类分类（INCART）实验，评估其在不同客户端配置下的性能表现。

Result: 在MIT-BIH数据集上，HQKAN减少37.35%可训练参数，通信成本降低24.89%；在INCART数据集上，分别降低44.81%和36.41%；同时提升整体与少数类别分类性能，证明其在通信效率与鲁棒性上的优势。

Conclusion: HQKAN是一种紧凑、通信高效且对跨客户端分布变化具有鲁棒性的模型，是隐私保护联邦学习中处理生物信号数据的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) recordings are sensitive biomedical data, limiting the ability of hospitals and wearable devices to share raw signals for centralized model training. Federated learning addresses this practical privacy constraint by enabling collaborative model training while keeping raw biosignal data at their respective sources. However, federated ECG classification remains challenging due to limited client-side samples, imbalanced arrhythmia labels, and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data across clients. These constraints require classifiers that are both communication-efficient and robust to cross-client distribution shifts. In this work, we evaluate a hybrid quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold network (HQKAN) against a multilayer perceptron (MLP) for five-class arrhythmia classification on the MIT-BIH dataset and three-class classification on the INCART dataset under federated averaging (FedAvg). Across multiple client configurations, HQKAN improves most aggregate and minority-class metrics while using 37.35% fewer trainable parameters and reducing communication cost by 24.89% on MIT-BIH; on INCART, it achieves corresponding reductions of 44.81% and 36.41%. These results indicate that HQKAN offers a compact, communication-efficient and robust alternative to the MLP baseline for privacy-aware federated learning on biosignal data.

</details>


### [214] [High-dimensional nonparametric changepoint detection via low-rank degree-two density projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13922)
*Guoqing Zhang,Zhaixin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于表示的高维分布变化检测方法，通过矩阵均值估计替代密度估计，利用秩-r截断的矩阵CUSUM扫描，捕捉低秩投影跳跃，具有非渐近的算子范数分析，适用于多变化点检测与交叉验证优化，实验表明在高维数据中计算高效且能检测均值CUSUM无法发现的依赖性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间中分布变化检测困难，尤其当前后分布均无参数形式时，传统方法难以有效捕捉复杂变化模式，尤其是依赖性变化。

Method: 构建对称特征矩阵 $H_2(X)$ 以编码密度的二阶正交投影，通过秩-$r$截断的矩阵CUSUM扫描检测变化点，结合交叉拟合标量精修与几何$\beta$-混合扩展，实现精确恢复与最优误差率。

Result: 该方法在高维（最高达200维）和多变化点场景下保持计算可行性，能准确检测均值不变但依赖结构变化的情形；误差率达到$\widetilde O_{\Pp}(κ^{-2})$，并有匹配的Le Cam下界证明其最优性。

Conclusion: 所提出的\LRD{}方法在无需密度参数假设的前提下，有效识别高维分布中的复杂变化，尤其擅长捕捉隐藏于均值之外的依赖性改变，在理论与实证上均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Detecting distributional changes in high dimension is difficult when neither the pre-change nor post-change density is parametrically specified. We introduce a representation-based approach that retains all degree-at-most-two density information while replacing density estimation by matrix mean estimation. For observations in $[-1,1]^d$, a symmetric feature matrix $H_2(X)\in\R^{(d+1)\times(d+1)}$ is constructed so that $M(f)=\E_f H_2(X)$ is an isometric encoding of the degree-two orthogonal projection of the density. We scan matrix CUSUMs after rank-$r$ truncation, exploiting the low rank of the projected jump rather than sparsity of individual coordinates. The resulting \LRD{} estimator has a tent-shaped population objective and a nonasymptotic operator-norm analysis whose leading stochastic term scales as $\sqrt{rd\log(nd)}$. For multiple changes, we give a seeded narrowest-over-threshold procedure and prove exact recovery by an induction that preserves an isolating interval for every undetected change. A cross-fitted scalar refinement learns the changing low-rank direction on one fold and localizes on the other, attaining $\widetilde O_{\Pp}(κ^{-2})$ error; a matching Le Cam lower bound shows optimality up to logarithms. A geometrically $β$-mixing extension follows from a dependent matrix Bernstein inequality. Experiments with ambient dimension up to $200$, a three-change $d=100$ sequence, and a $128$-feature human-activity benchmark show that the method remains computationally practical and accurately detects pure dependence changes that are invisible to mean CUSUMs.

</details>


### [215] [CForce: Boosting Parallel Decoding for dLLMs via Consistency Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13925)
*Yuji Ren,Chenkai Xu,Zhuocheng Gong,Jianguo Li,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Consistency Forcing (CForce)方法，通过强制早期去噪阶段的掩码预测与后期一致，提升扩散语言模型在高并行解码下的可靠性与性能。利用自回滚轨迹进行蒸馏训练，并引入置信度自适应KL散度作为目标函数，结合前向与后向KL的优点。理论分析表明该方法可近似最小化早期预测误差。适用于掩码到词和可编辑解码场景，尤其在高并行预算下显著改善速度-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型在高并行解码策略下，早期去噪阶段的预测不可靠，错误会传播至后续阶段，影响生成质量。需要一种机制来增强早期预测的稳定性与一致性。

Method: 提出CForce方法，基于预收集的自回滚轨迹进行蒸馏训练；采用置信度自适应KL散度作为损失函数，实现前后向KL的优势融合；提供理论分析说明其对减少早期预测误差的有效性。

Result: 在非编辑和可编辑的LLaDA模型上均取得改进，特别是在高并行解码预算下，显著提升了速度与质量之间的平衡。

Conclusion: CForce通过一致性强制有效提升了扩散语言模型在高并行解码下的生成稳定性与准确性，具有广泛适用性且可扩展至多种解码模式。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate language generation by predicting multiple masks in a single forward pass. However, existing dLLMs can suffer from unreliable predictions in early denoising stages under aggressive parallelism strategies, leading to errors that can propagate to later stages. To tackle this issue, we present Consistency Forcing (CForce) for dLLMs, a distillation method to force the mask predictions of early stages to align with those of later stages. CForce trains the model on pre-collected self-rollout trajectories, thereby improving training-inference alignment. We introduce Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence as a distillation objective to conjoin the merits of forward and reverse KL.
